| WI10 | [Yours] I point the Projects step at my ground itself. | §10.11 Projects step · Settings window, Setup page | Central | The step says there is nothing to make. | Nothing is made twice. | P |
| WI11 | [Arriving] Before I accept a project I want to read exactly what will happen. | §10.11 Projects step, Preview · Settings window, Setup page | Central | Preview lists every step Central would take, in order. | Nothing changes until I hold Accept. | P |
| WI12 | [Arriving] I stop Setup halfway and come back to it another day. | §10.11 · Settings window, Setup page | Central, AIKit and Workcell | What I accepted stays in force. Running Setup again makes nothing twice. | Accept and Remove stay held throughout. | M |
| WI13 | [Yours] My setup predates this installation, and I have local edits in it. | §10.11 · Settings window, Setup page | Central, AIKit and Workcell | Setup brings the setup into agreement and keeps my local edits beside what it proposes. | Nothing I have moves, changes or is deleted before I accept. | M |
| WI14 | [Lived-in] I find another folder of work later and want it in my world. | §10.3 Places, §16.7 · the browser, Work place, Add folder… | Central | Setup's Projects step opens for that folder. | Dropping the folder on a track instead never sets it up. | M |

## WS — Change how my world works in one window

**Why:** "molding to the user's uniqueness in terms of their setup" (UX chat 27 Oct).
**Where:** manual §10.10 · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25 (the shell's grammar)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WS01 | [Lived-in] I need to change a setting without hunting through each owner's files. | §10.10 · Settings window, on ⌘, or Settings… in the application's menu | The shell's Settings | Settings opens in a window of its own, its pages down the left. Esc returns me to the work I came from. | Settings never takes the main area. It is no device, pane or widget. | P |
| WS02 | [Lived-in] I know a word of a setting's name but not its page. | §10.10 Search · Settings window | The shell's Settings | I type the word, press a result and land on the setting, or beside the instrument a device's parameter belongs to. | — | P |
| WS03 | [Lived-in] I need a change to hold for one project, not for my whole machine. | §10.10 Applies to · Settings window | Each owner reads its settings at the chosen scope | With Applies to set to this machine, the world, a project or a workcell, each row shows its value at that scope, and From says whether it is set here or inherited. | — | P |
| WS04 | [Lived-in] I change a value and need it to stay mine until I have read what it will do. | §10.10 A setting's row · Settings window | The shell's Settings keeps the change local | The row carries an amber mark; the page's name and the window's foot count my changes. | Nothing is applied yet. Discard drops every change. | P |
| WS05 | [Lived-in] I want each owner to tell me what my changes will do before anything applies. | §10.10 Review changes… · Settings window, Review page | Each owner plans its change | Each plan shows its scope, the change and when it takes effect: next resolution, new sessions, reconnect or restart. | — | P |
| WS06 | [Lived-in] I apply my reviewed changes. | §10.10 Apply · Settings window, Review page | Each owner applies its own plan | Each owner answers in the same table: applied, verified or refused, with its reason. | A refused change stays mine with the owner's reason in its own words. A running session keeps its harness, its model and its composition. | P |
| WS07 | [Lived-in] An owner's reading moved under a change I had made. | §10.10 Keep · Settings window, Review page | The owner | Keep holds my change on the new reading. | Discard drops it instead. | P |
| WS08 | [Lived-in] I want a value back to what the owner would resolve without me. | §10.10 Reset · a setting's row | The owner resolves the value again | The row shows the inherited value. | Reset stands only where this scope sets the value. | P |

## WP — Choose the harnesses, keys, machines and routines my work runs on

**Why:** "the OI products, which are, you know, the technical system underneath the modes of the DLW" (UX chat 27 Oct).
**Where:** manual §10.10 (the pages) · **Port sources:** none named in #714

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WP01 | [Arriving] I want new chats to start with a particular harness, and each harness to request a particular model. | §10.10 Harnesses & models page · Settings window | O:I's chat default, AIKit | New sessions start with that harness and model. Each harness client shows installed, detected or why not, with Install, Folder and Sign in. Where a harness has its own login, Sign in runs it in a terminal where I answer it; a harness that signs in only inside itself says so and offers no button. | A running session keeps the harness and model it started with. A harness whose sign-in cannot be read loses only its own Sign in. | M |
| WP02 | [Lived-in] Before I rely on a model route, I need to know how far it has been proven. | §10.10 Harnesses & models page · Settings window | AIKit | Each route of the catalogue shows its standing: unproven, observed or usable. | — | P |
| WP03 | [Lived-in] I choose which skill-sets new work is offered by default. | §10.10 Skills & methods page · Settings window | AIKit, Central | New work composes the sets I chose, and each skill shows its standing. A skill that declares settings of its own shows them under it, and each skill shows what it adds to an agent's context. | The page shows only when an owner contributes a setting to it. | P |
| WP04 | [Lived-in] I keep my current defaults under a name and start a building day from them. | §10.10 Profiles page: New from changes, Use, Import · Settings window | The shell, AIKit | New from changes keeps my changes as a profile. Use stages a profile's settings as local changes for review. Import reads a profile file. | A staged profile applies nothing until I review and apply it. | P |
| WP05 | [Lived-in] I decide whether what I say to an agent may leave this machine. | §10.10 Permissions page · Settings window | AIKit, Central, Actuation | Local only, Ask or Allowed holds for new work. Each harness's starting permission mode and the owners' access policies stand beside it. | The owners' access policies are read only here. | P |
| WP06 | [Arriving] I check a provider binding, and rotate or revoke its key. | §10.10 Credentials page: Verify, Rotate, Revoke, Discover, Add binding · Settings window | AIKit, Workcell | Each binding shows its secret reference, its state and its last verdict. A key I enter goes to the computer's own secure field and on to the credential owner. Beside a provider's key stands its own login, where it has one, so I can sign in instead of entering a key. | No value is ever shown. Discover reports where a key lies, never what it holds. Revoke is held. | M |
| WP07 | [Lived-in] I want to know what uses each secret Workcell holds, and where it has been projected. | §10.10 Credentials page: Scan, Revoke · Settings window | Workcell | Each secret reference lists what uses it, and its projections stand with it. | Scan changes no setting. Revoke is held. | M |
| WP08 | [Lived-in] I add a machine a workcell can carry work to, or disconnect one. | §10.10 Machines & services page: Add machine, Connect, Disconnect, Remove · Settings window | Workcell, AIKit | Each machine shows its connection and what it was granted. The services the workcell declares and the local services AIKit elects stand below. | Disconnect and Remove are held. | M |
| WP09 | [Lived-in] I want a verified Method to run by itself on a schedule or an event. | §10.10 Automations page: Create routine, Enable, Run now, Disable, Reprove · Settings window | AIKit | The routine shows its state, its next run and whether it runs unattended. Show lists routines, Methods, harness timers not yet adopted, or the run history. | Run now and Disable are held. This is the one place a run is given a time: no device, pad or Day move keeps one. A duplicate or missed trigger, sleep and wake, a revoked grant or a stale Method each has stated behaviour and repeats no effect; useful findings reach the place I chose, and routine runs do not ask for my attention. Disabling a routine stops its next runs; a run already under way finishes or is stopped on its own. | P |
| WP10 | [Lived-in] I change how the window looks. | §10.10 Appearance page: Apply, Revert, Import, Remove · Settings window | O:I's presentation | Light, Dark, Follow system or a library theme applies, with my imported themes listed beside them, and the visual layer and the welcome scene set as I chose. | Revert returns the theme I had. | P |
| WP11 | [Lived-in] A workcell does not hold what it declares, and I need to see why. | §10.10 System page · Settings window | Each product | Each owner's reading stands on four axes, declared, effective, active and staged, with the drift between two of them; or the owner's read-only configuration. | An axis an owner cannot read says why when I point at it. | M |
| WP12 | [Lived-in] I set Factory's search limits and watch interval, and the Central project each register binds to. | §10.10 Search & telemetry and Sources & libraries pages · Settings window | Factory | New work uses the limits and bindings I set. | These pages show only when Factory contributes a setting to them. | P |

## WN — Hold the window the same way in every kind of work

**Why:** "Just in the same way Ableton Live is professional software, it's not easy to use, but once you get your grips on it, it's incredibly fun" (UX chat 27 Oct).
**Where:** manual §10.1, §10.8, §16.4 · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25 (session model, object model); interior.dev (the shell's kit)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WN01 | [Lived-in] I need more room for the work, so I hide the browser, then bring it back. | §10.1 Showing, hiding and sizing · the browser | The shell | ⌥⌘B, ⌥⌘5 or the toggle at the top left hides it and the main area takes its room. Shown again, it returns at the size I left; I size it by dragging its right edge. | — | P |
| WN02 | [Lived-in] I want the pool in view, and more height for it. | §10.1, §16.4 · the pool | The shell | ⌥⌘6, or its toggle at the foot of the browser, shows it. Dragging the line between it and the browser gives either the height. | — | P |
| WN03 | [Lived-in] I hide or show the widget rail and set how wide it stands. | §10.1 · the widget rail | The shell | ⌥⌘8, or its toggle at the top right, hides or shows it; dragging its left edge sizes it. | Hidden, it comes back at the width I left. | P |
| WN04 | [Lived-in] I want the selected clip and the chain working on it, one above the other, or only one of them. | §10.1 The bottom right, §16.4 · Clip view, Device view | The shell | ⌥-click on Clip or Device shows both. ⌥⌘3 and ⌥⌘4, or ▲ beside each switch, show or hide one. ⇧Tab switches when only one shows. ⌥⌘L hides both or brings them back. | — | P |
| WN05 | [Lived-in] I need to reach one device on a long chain quickly. | §10.1 The bottom right · the Device switch, Device view | The shell | Right-clicking the Device switch lists the chain's devices; choosing one goes to it. | — | P |
| WN06 | [Lived-in] I want to see every track's mixer row, or only some of its sections. | §10.1, §10.8, §16.6 · Session view, Arrangement view | The shell | ⌥⌘M, or Mixer at the bottom right, shows or hides the mixer row. ⌥⌘I, ⌥⌘S and ⌥⌘R show or hide the In/Out section, the sends and the return tracks. | A mode draws only the sections its tracks have. | P |
| WN07 | [Lived-in] I want the window to fill the screen. | §10.1, §10.8 · the window | The shell | ⌃⌘F lets the window fill the screen, and again returns it. | — | P |
| WN08 | [Lived-in] I move focus between parts of the window from the keyboard. | §10.2, §10.8 · every part | The shell | ⌥0 to the transport, ⌥1 Session view, ⌥2 Arrangement view, ⌥3 Clip view, ⌥4 Device view, ⌥5 the browser, ⌥6 the pool, ⌥9 Pane view. The part with focus has a bright edge, and the keys act on what is selected in it. | A drag, an open menu, a value I am typing and Clip view expanded into the main area last only while I use them. | P |
| WN09 | [Lived-in] I need to know what a control does, or why something did not happen. | §10.1, §10.9 · the status bar | The shell, carrying the owner's word | The status bar's one line says what the control under the pointer does, or what went wrong. | An act its owner cannot take says there which operation it waits for, and whose. | P |
| WN10 | [Lived-in] A view is still loading, or its owner cannot be reached, and I need to know what I am looking at. | §10.2 The three views side by side · Session view, Arrangement view, Pane view | The shell, reading each owner | What was last read stands at once, dimmed, until the owner answers; a pane shows its device's last picture, dimmed. | While the owner cannot be reached nothing launches and the device's light is dark. When something fails it stays as last read, and one line in the status bar says what failed. | P |
| WN11 | [Lived-in] I close the window while an agent works in a terminal, and come back later. | §10.1 Where a session lives · a session space | AIKit: keeps the session space | The work goes on in its room while the window is closed; reopened, I come back to the same room and the same agent. | A space is a room, not a self: the agent working in it is the same wherever the room is shown. | M |
| WN12 | [Lived-in] I show the same session space through herdr and through tmux, and one of them stops answering. | §10.1 Where a session lives · a session space | AIKit | Each provider shows the space under its own name, and the names are labels, never who the space is. | If one provider stops answering, only its view of the space goes dark. | M |
| WN13 | [Lived-in] I write to an agent while it works in its room. | §10.1, §10.6 · Messages on the rail | AIKit, through the Gateway | My message reaches the agent through Messages. A Terminal I open follows the room of the workcell its track works in. | A message never types into the agent's room. | P |
| WN14 | [Lived-in] I type in a document and must not trigger anything by accident. | §10.8 · any document | The shell | While I type, keys never play notes and single letters never act. | — | P |
| WN15 | [Lived-in] The machine that holds my agent's room restarts, and I come back to the work. | §10.1 Where a session lives · a session space | AIKit (the session space), with Workcell | I am told what came back: the room's layout, the history on its screen, the agent's own session taken up again, or a fresh agent given the same work; the work keeps its identity whichever it is. | A room rebuilt after a restart is never shown as the old one still running, and a name or a number that turns up again does not make it the same room. | M |

## WV — Move between the grid, the ruler and the panes without losing my place

**Why:** "a way to transcribe and translate between different mediums, and essentially the next evolution of one's creativity is always available within the same app" (UX chat 27 Oct).
**Where:** manual §10.2, §4, §16.6 · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25 (session model); interior.dev (the shell's kit)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WV01 | [Lived-in] I launch something in the grid and want to follow its course on the ruler. | §10.2 · Tab, Session view and Arrangement view | The shell | Tab switches views. The selection stays; what runs keeps running; a selected clip with a place on the ruler is selected and scrolled into sight. | Where the clip has no place yet, as with a task that has not run, its track's head lights. | P |
| WV02 | [Lived-in] I want the instruments I work in at full size, then the grid again. | §10.2 · Pane view | The shell | ⌥⌘9 shows Pane view, and again returns to the view I left. From Pane view, Tab returns to the view I left. | Switching changes neither the selection nor what runs. | P |
| WV03 | [Lived-in] I want the grid on one screen and the ruler or the panes on the other. | §10.2 · a second window | The shell | ⇧⌘W opens a second window showing its own view of the same set. | — | P |
| WV04 | [Lived-in] I need the whole width to edit a clip. | §10.2 · Clip view expanded into the main area | The shell | ⌥⌘E lets Clip view fill the main area; pressed again, the view I left comes back as it was. | Only Clip view can take the main area from the three views. | P |
| WV05 | [Lived-in] I open an instrument to work in. | §10.2 Pane view · Pane view | The shell, with the instrument's owner | Dragging the instrument from the browser into a pane opens it there; clicking it opens it in the pane that has focus. | An empty pane waits for an instrument from the browser. | P |
| WV06 | [Lived-in] I want a document beside the day it belongs to. | §10.2 Pane view · ⊞ | The shell | ⊞ splits the pane Beside, Below or Four ways. | — | P |
| WV07 | [Lived-in] I rearrange what stands in my panes. | §10.2 Pane view · a pane's tab | The shell | Dragging a tab onto the centre of another pane moves it there; dragging it onto a pane's edge makes a new split. | — | P |
| WV08 | [Lived-in] I want one pane to fill the area for a while. | §10.2 Pane view · a pane's tab | The shell | Double-clicking the tab fills the main area with it; again brings the other panes back. | — | P |
| WV09 | [Lived-in] I want a pane to keep its subject while I select other things. | §10.2 Pane view · a pinned pane | The shell | A pinned pane keeps its subject while I work elsewhere. | Unpinned, it follows my selection. | P |
| WV10 | [Lived-in] I focus a pane and want its controls. | §10.2 Pane view · Device view under the pane | The shell, with the device's owner | Device view shows the chain the device stands on, with the device selected. Something I select inside the instrument that is a clip, such as a stop of a walk, becomes the mode's selected clip. | A device that stands on no chain shows in Device view alone. | P |
| WV11 | [Lived-in] I change an instrument to another of its builds. | §10.2 Pane view · the instrument in its pane | The shell, with the instrument's owner | The other build answers to the same controls, and my settings stay. | — | P |
| WV12 | [Lived-in] I need to read at a glance where everything on the grid stands. | §3, §16.6 Session view · Session view | The shell, reading each clip's owner | Each clip shows its state: waiting blinks fast and has no length, running blinks slowly with no end drawn, stopped stands ready, recording carries the record light, stalled is striped and frozen where its output stopped, done is dimmed. | A stalled clip never looks done; its track lights a retry. | P |
| WV13 | [Lived-in] I fold a track to one line, or open its lanes and its take lanes. | §10.8, §16.6 Arrangement view · Arrangement view | The shell | U folds or opens the selected track; ⌥⌘U shows or hides the take lanes. | A fold is not a change to undo. | P |
| WV14 | [Lived-in] I change where clips stand in time on the ruler. | §16.6 Arrangement view, §10.8 · Arrangement view | The shell, with the mode's owner | Move (insert or overwrite), Trim, Roll, Slip, Slide, Split (⌘E), Consolidate (⌘J) and Crop (⇧⌘J) each change only where a clip stands in time; Snap aligns edges, and Alt held while dragging turns it off. | Every edit is one labelled step that undoes alone. A move never raises a claim to a fact. | P |
| WV15 | [Lived-in] I step back into a past moment, a chosen day's sky, and compose there. | §4, §17.2 The sky and its occasions · Pane view | Quaternal Logic (the sky), with the shell | The sky and the instruments stand in that moment while I compose in Clip view. | Tap returns me to now. | P |
| WV16 | [Lived-in] I come back to a pane I left a moment ago, from another mode or another set. | §10.2 The three views side by side · Pane view | The shell | The pane is there at once as I left it, with its scroll, its selection and the page's own state; nothing is read again because I looked away. | What changed while I was away is read again in place, the last reading standing until the new one arrives; a slow folder never holds back a page I already know. | P |
| WV17 | [Lived-in] The DLW stops without warning while I am writing, and I open it again. | §10.2 The three views side by side, What is kept · the main area | The shell, with each page's owner | The set and its panes come back, and the writing the DLW had kept on this machine comes back on its page as unsaved, beside the revision it began from. | A part of the saved window that cannot be read is set aside with its reason and the rest opens; it is never replaced by an empty window, and a change made elsewhere meanwhile shows as a conflict. | M |

## WT — Use the same transport in every kind of work

**Why:** "it implies that there is translatability between musical, visual, pure informational, and agentic modes of work" (UX chat 27 Oct).
**Where:** manual §10.1 The transport, §10.5, §10.7, §16.6 · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25 (session model); interior.dev (the shell's kit)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WT01 | [Lived-in] I change the kind of work I am doing. | §10.1, §3 · the transport, Mode | The shell | The main area, the browser's lead and each control's meaning follow the new mode, and what I selected stays selected. | Changing mode closes nothing, and a crossing never sends, shows or launches anything by itself. | P |
| WT02 | [Lived-in] I reach for a control where it always stands, in a mode that has no use for it. | §10.1 The transport · the transport | The shell | Every control has one place, the same in every mode, from Browser at the left to Rail at the right. | A control the mode does not use stays in its place, dimmed, and nothing slides into its space. | P |
| WT03 | [Lived-in] I need to know what I have selected and where it belongs. | §10.1 · the title line | The shell | The title line names the selection, its world and its day, with Back and Forward at its head; the moment I stand in shows in Position. | — | P |
| WT04 | [Lived-in] I go back to where I was, and forward again. | §10.7 · Back and Forward on the title line | The shell, with the trail Central keeps | I return to the same place, the same page and the same point in the reading. Forward goes the other way until I go somewhere new. | Changing kind of work never closes anything, and every place can be linked to and opened again. | P |
| WT05 | [Lived-in] I play or stop whatever is in front of me, and cancel at once when I must. | §10.8, §16.6 Rules · Space, Play, Stop | The shell, with the mode's owner | Space plays or stops the transport in every view. | Stop pressed twice cancels at once; launch quantize does not delay it. | P |
| WT06 | [Lived-in] I set when launches land for every clip that follows the transport. | §10.1, §16.6 Session view · Quantize | The shell | The transport's Quantize is the launch quantize of every clip set to Global. | — | P |
| WT07 | [Lived-in] I set a stretch of the ruler to repeat, or to record in. | §10.1 Loop group, §16.6 · the loop brace, Punch in, Punch out | The shell, with the mode's owner | The transport plays and repeats only between the loop's in and out points; recording happens only between punch in and punch out (in Factory, agents' writing). | A punch marks a stretch of the ruler, never a clock time. | P |
| WT08 | [Lived-in] I took hold of a value something else was moving, and want to give it back. | §10.1, §16.6 · Re-enable | The shell, with the owner of what was holding it | Re-enable returns control of the value to the recorded moves, the agent or the instrument that was holding it. | A value I override gives control back only when I press Re-enable. | P |
| WT09 | [Lived-in] I want a key, a phrase or a MIDI control to work a control. | §10.1 Hands group, §10.5 · Key map, MIDI map | The shell | The control answers to what I mapped to it, in every mode. | Keys never play or act while I type in a document. | P |
| WT10 | [Lived-in] I draw how a parameter moves over the clips of a track. | §10.8, §16.6 Arrangement view · Automation Mode (A), automation lanes | The shell, with the device's owner | A gives the track's head a device chooser and a parameter chooser; the parameter's moves lie over the clips and I draw them. + opens the parameter in a lane of its own; arming the track and moving the parameter records the move. | − on a lane hides it and keeps its moves. | P |

## WA — Act on anything as Live acts on it

**Why:** "the system is built around this plug-and-play devices and instruments ontology from Ableton" (UX chat 27 Oct).
**Where:** manual §3, §10.9, §16.1 (Live's acts, kept as Live keeps them), §16.5, §16.6 · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25 (session model, object model)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WA01 | [Lived-in] I want to see everything I can do to a clip. | §10.9 · a clip's menu, by right-click, the menu key or ⇧F10 | The shell, asking the mode's owner | The menu lists Launch and Stop, Cut, Copy, Duplicate, Delete, Rename, Switch off or on, Edit info text and a colour from Live's palette, each with its key; in Arrangement view, Split, Consolidate and Crop. | An act its owner cannot take stays dimmed. Delete and every act that takes something away is drawn in the record colour. | P |
| WA02 | [Lived-in] I rename a clip. | §10.9 · ⌘R, through Clip view | The mode's owner, through the revision Clip view holds | The name changes; Tab keeps it and moves on to the next. The owner's answer shows on the line under the grid or the ruler. | If Clip view is hidden it comes forward first. A refusal shows in the owner's own words. On a track read from another reading, the act says it would change that reading. | P |
| WA03 | [Lived-in] I switch a clip off without losing it. | §10.9, §16.5 · 0 | The mode's owner | The clip shows grey and does not launch; I can still edit it. | With nothing selected that can be switched off, 0 launches the tenth row. | P |
| WA04 | [Lived-in] I start a new clip in an empty slot. | §10.9, §16.6 Session view · an empty slot: double-click, Insert clip (⇧⌘M), Paste (⌘V) | The mode's owner | A clip stands in the slot, ready to write. | — | P |
| WA05 | [Lived-in] I record straight into an empty slot of a track I have armed. | §10.9 · an empty slot: Record here | The mode's owner | What I record becomes the slot's clip. | Record here stands only on an armed track. | P |
| WA06 | [Lived-in] I stop what plays on one track. | §10.9, §16.6 · an empty slot's stop square | The mode's owner | The track's clip stops. Remove stop button (⌘E) takes the square out of a slot. | — | P |
| WA07 | [Lived-in] I act on a whole track. | §10.9 · a track's menu | The mode's owner | Rename, Duplicate, Delete, Switch off or on, Solo, Arm, Group (⌘G), Fold (U), Move left (⌘←) and right (⌘→), and a colour. | Solo and Arm wait in the menu where the instrument keeps their letter keys. | P |
| WA08 | [Lived-in] I group tracks so a team of them reads as one. | §10.9, §16.6 The track · ⌘G | The mode's owner | The group folds under one header with one coloured rail for each level, and a row in the group launches all its children. | — | P |
| WA09 | [Lived-in] I set several things going as one move. | §3, §10.9 · a row (a stop): Launch (Enter), 1 to 9, the Main track's row launcher | The mode's owner | Every clip in the row launches at once. Insert row (⌘I), Rename, Duplicate, Delete and Stop all clips stand in its menu. | A row launches only what is in it. | P |
| WA10 | [Lived-in] I launch one clip. | §10.8, §16.6 · Enter, or the clip's ▶ | The mode's owner | The clip plays through its track's chain and shows its state. | A deactivated clip does not launch. | P |
| WA11 | [Lived-in] I want things to run on without pressing each one. | §3, §16.5 Launch · Clip view, Launch tab, follow action | The mode's owner | The clip's follow action decides what comes after it on its own track, with what chance. | A follow action never moves to another track. | P |
| WA12 | [Lived-in] I set how one clip launches and when its launch lands. | §3, §16.5 Launch · Clip view, Launch tab | The mode's owner | Trigger, Gate, Toggle or Repeat, and its own quantize, or Global to follow the transport. | A clip placed rather than launched, such as a Central page, has no Launch tab. | P |
| WA13 | [Lived-in] I choose the take that stands from several under a clip. | §3, §16.6 Take lanes · Arrangement view | The mode's owner | Auditioning a take from its lane, selecting a stretch and pressing Return copies it into the main lane, which shows which take each stretch came from. | — | P |
| WA14 | [Lived-in] I take back the last change I made through an act. | §10.9 · ⌘Z, ⇧⌘Z | The owner that made the change | Each step is named as its act was, such as "Rename Bollingen", and undoes alone; Redo makes it again. | A fold, a view switched or a pane split is not undone. While I type in a field, Undo and Redo are the field's own. | P |
| WA15 | [Lived-in] I look for an act I do not know the key for. | §10.9 · the menu bar | The shell | Every act has one home in the menu bar: Edit holds Undo, Redo, Cut, Copy, Paste, Duplicate, Delete and Rename; Create holds Insert Clip and Insert Row; View holds the views and the parts of the window. | — | P |

## WB — Find anything I can work with and bring it in

**Why:** "you open the app, you are essentially presented with all the possibilities and your existing work and yourself alongside that" (UX chat 27 Oct).
**Where:** manual §10.3, §16.7 · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25 (session model); interior.dev (the shell's kit)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WB01 | [Lived-in] I want to see what something is before I commit to it. | §10.3, §16.7 · the browser, preview at the foot of the list | The shell, reading the row's owner | Selecting a row once shows its preview at the foot of the list: a page's first lines, a material's picture, a device's account of what it does, a chat's last exchange. | A preview changes nothing; opening and adding are separate acts. | P |
| WB02 | [Lived-in] I open material to work in it. | §10.3, §16.7 Open · the browser, Pane view | The material's owner | Double-clicking it, or dragging it into a pane, opens it in its instrument. | — | P |
| WB03 | [Lived-in] I bring material into the set I am working in. | §10.3, §16.7 Bring into the set · the browser, an empty track | The mode's owner | Dropped on an empty track it joins the set: a world joins today, a chat's session reopens where it stopped. | A drop admits material and never sets anything up; setup is its own act. | P |
| WB04 | [Lived-in] I start a new track with an instrument. | §3, §10.3, §16.7 Start a track · the browser, an empty track | The mode's owner | An instrument, or one of its presets, dropped on an empty track starts a track with it; an agent dropped there takes the seat. | — | P |
| WB05 | [Lived-in] I add a device to a chain, or keep a monitor at hand. | §10.3, §16.7 Add · the browser, Device view, the widget rail | The device's owner | The device stands where I dropped it: on the chain, or as a tile on the rail. | A monitor never stands on a chain. | P |
| WB06 | [Lived-in] I want a running agent, session or workcell at hand while I work. | §10.3, §16.7 Keep at hand · the browser's Running things, the widget rail | The running thing's owner | Selecting it follows it; dragged onto the rail, its tile opens there; a chat dragged onto the rail is a place to talk in it. | — | P |
| WB07 | [Lived-in] I keep the things I reach for most as coloured favourites. | §10.3 · the browser, Collections | The shell | My Collections stand at the head of the browser's left side, in every mode. | — | P |
| WB08 | [Lived-in] I look for what I brought in at setup where I would expect it. | §10.3 Places · the browser: You, Agents, Machines, Work | Central, AIKit, Workcell | My ground and identity under You, my agents with their harnesses and skills under Agents, my machines and their workcells under Machines, my projects under Work. | A credential is never in the browser. Settings shows its reference, never its value. | P |
| WB09 | [Lived-in] I change mode and need the browser to lead with what that work is made of. | §10.3, §16.7 · the browser | The shell, with each mode's owners | Live leads with my sets and the Torus's saved sounds; Central with my whole field undivided by project, Earth, the worlds I visit and the earlier Days; Factory with my projects, each opening to its chats, newest first; Expressions with my Expressions and their scenes, then the library; Technē with my sources, then the bodies, places, palaces and journeys of the world I stand in. | The other kinds follow in the same order in every mode, and no mode adds a browser of its own. | P |

## WD — Shape what works on the material with devices

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| WD02 | [Lived-in] I read a device at a glance while my attention is on the material. | §10.4, §16.3 · Device view: Folded, Compact | The device's owner | Folded, it shows its name and a reading or two in a narrow strip; compact, a small live picture and up to four controls. | A change in any view shows in the others at once. | P |
| WD03 | [Lived-in] I need every setting of one device. | §10.4, §16.4 · ▸ on its head; the expanded band above the chain | The device's owner | Its Whole view stands as a band above the chain, every setting grouped round a larger picture; the panes above shrink and nothing is covered. | One device stands expanded at a time. Esc or ▾ hides it. | P |
| WD04 | [Lived-in] I want a device's material full size, to work in. | §10.4, §16.4 · ▦ on its head; Pane view | The device's owner | The device opens full in the pane that has focus, with its controls staying in Device view below. | A device with no material of its own has no Full. | P |
| WD05 | [Lived-in] I want a device in a window of its own, on another screen. | §10.4, §16.3, §16.4 · ↗ on its head; its own window | The device's owner | The window holds the material with every setting under it, and can fill the screen. Any number of windows can stay open, each a view of the same device: what I change in one shows on the chain at once, and closing it leaves the device as it stands. | A window follows its device: when the device leaves the chain, its window closes. | P |
| WD06 | [Lived-in] I fold a device I am not using to its title. | §10.4, §16.4 · double-click the head, or Fold in its menu | The shell | The device stands folded to its title on the chain; pressing the title opens it again. | — | P |
| WD07 | [Lived-in] I switch a device off and on. | §10.4, §16.4 Activator · the device's head | The device's owner | The activator asks the owner to turn it on or off, and the light shows what the owner reports. | If the state is not known, the light stays dark. | P |
| WD08 | [Lived-in] I choose which chain of a rack plays, and turn several settings with one knob. | §10.4, §16.4 · a rack's selector, its macros, the three marks on its head | The rack's owner | The selector chooses between chains; a macro moves several settings at once, each within its own range, and its letter key turns it; the head's marks show or hide the macros, the chain list and the selected chain's devices. | — | P |
| WD09 | [Lived-in] I need to know what moved a value, and to take it over. | §10.4, §16.1 · the value on the device | The owner of what moved it | The value shows what moved it: an agent's edit carries the agent's name; a knob the sky turns shows its planet. Taking hold of it lights the hand-back light. | Pressing the hand-back light, or Re-enable, gives control back; a modulator lets go until then. | P |
| WD10 | [Lived-in] I keep a device's settings to start from again. | §16.4 · the head's menu: Save as preset | The device's owner | The preset stands under its device in the browser. | — | P |
| WD11 | [Lived-in] I replace a device on a chain with another in its place. | §16.4 · the head's menu: Hot-swap | The device's owner | The new device stands where the old one stood on the chain. | — | P |

## WO — Stand in my world and see how far it reaches

**Why:** "This is why the whole thing's based around world-building. It's there to reflect and help refine the reflection of one's world." (UX chat 27 Oct). "the personal being the collective, and vice versa" (UX chat 27 Oct).
**Where:** manual §2, §17.2 (Your system, Aperture and view as); #716 · **Port sources:** MapLibre GL JS (Earth); Archetypal Earth, ported as code

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WO01 | [Bare] I open My World and see it from close orbit. | §2, §17.2 Your system · Pane view, the World | The shell (Technē's World), reading my ground | A planet turns under its star with my agents at work in orbit and what they just made on a wider ring. A world I have barely begun is a small grey moon. | Nothing but agents and what they make circles it; my files and open pages are on the ground. | P |
| WO02 | [Lived-in] I see my projects as places on my world. | §2, §17.2 Your system · Pane view, the World | The shell (Technē's World), with Central | Each project I brought in or admitted to today takes a body and a landing place; the more I live in it, the more finished it looks. | The look is read from how long the world has existed, how much it holds and how often I shaped it, never stored; none of these ever makes it less. | P |
| WO03 | [Lived-in] I widen how far I look, a step at a time. | §2, §17.2 Aperture · Pane view, the World | The shell (Technē's World) | My world alone, then with its projects and agents, then the shared field, then the fields it is joined to. | What lies just past the edge is counted and offered. | P |
| WO04 | [Lived-in] I look at my world as the field and the public see it before I show anything. | §2, §17.2 View as · Pane view, the World | The shell, with Central | As myself, as the field or as the public; whatever I have not shown lies under cloud. | Raising anything into wider view asks me first. | P |
| WO05 | [Lived-in] I visit someone else's world. | §2, §17.2 · Pane view, the World | The shell, with the other world's owner | I land on what they have shown, tinted and clouded as they left it, with the agent that keeps it there to talk to. | Findable is not reachable, reachable is not in touch, in touch is not trusted; each step is the owner's to give. | P |
| WO06 | [Lived-in] The other world's agent gives me something. | §2, §17.2 · my world | Central | It arrives in my world under their name. | It stays theirs until I make it my own. | P |
| WO07 | [Lived-in] I stand under my world's sky as it is today, or as it was on a day I choose. | §2, §17.2 The sky and its occasions · Pane view | Quaternal Logic (the sky) | The planets stand as the real ones do, now or at the occasion I chose. | The sky comes from its owner; if the owner is not available, the sky layer says why and draws nothing in its place. Tap returns to now. | P |

## FC — Carry one thing across the modes and let each make its own of it

**Why:** "the wiki constellations are what persist, the modes make the data of the constellations visible in their different modes" (#709). "you don't have to do anything other than transcribe, which is just shifting to something implicit, not recreating or reprocessing" (#709).
**Where:** #709 → manual §3, §10.3, §16.5, §17.1, §18.1, §19.1, §20.1, §21.1, §21.2 · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25 (session model: a sample as an audio clip's source, Show in Browser, the browser's preview)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FC01 | [Lived-in] I select a clip and need to know what it is an expression of. | #709 → §16.5 · Clip view, Clip panel | AIKit (the wiki constellation), read by the mode's owner | The Clip panel names the clip's constellation as its source, as an audio clip's panel names its sample, in whichever mode the clip stands. | A source that cannot be read keeps its last reading, dimmed, and the status bar says why. | P |
| FC02 | [Lived-in] I choose Show in Browser on that clip, then change mode. | #709 → §10.3 · Clip view, the browser | The shell | The browser holds the same constellation selected in the new mode, so I arrive with the same thing in front of me. | Changing mode keeps my selection and closes nothing; the crossing sends, shows and launches nothing by itself. | P |
| FC03 | [Lived-in] With the constellation selected, I read the browser's preview to see what this mode makes of it before I bring it in. | #709 → §10.3 · the browser's preview | The mode's owner | The preview shows this mode's form of the thing: Central's writing about it, Factory's work on it, Expressions' symbolic form, Technē's lensing, Live's sound. | The preview changes nothing; the constellation stays as it is. | H (core) |
| FC04 | [Lived-in] In Central, I drop the constellation on a track to write about it. | #709 → §18.1 · Central, Session view | Central | The track's clip is a page whose source is the constellation, open for writing in Clip view. | A node needs no file: the page is one of its expressions, and writing it leaves the constellation as it is. | P |
| FC05 | [Lived-in] In Factory, I drop the constellation on a session's track to bring it into agentic work. | #709 → §19.1 · Factory, Session view | Software Factory | The track's clip is a task whose source is the constellation; firing it runs the task. | — | P |
| FC06 | [Lived-in] In Expressions, I drop the constellation on a track to give it an expressive symbolic form. | #709 → §20.1 · Expressions, Session view | The shell (Expressions) | The track's clip is a scene whose source is the constellation: particles, glyphs and text overlays, cut as scenes, with its sound on the same timeline. | — | M |
| FC07 | [Lived-in] In Technē, I drop the constellation on a track to lens and refine it with Technē's tools. | #709 → §17.1 · Technē, Session view | The shell (Technē) | The track's clip is Technē's clip of it, whose source is the constellation, ready for the Lens and the other tools on the reading's chain. | — | P |
| FC08 | [Lived-in] In Live, I drop the constellation on a track to hear it. | #709 → §21.1, §21.2 · Live, Session view | The shell (Live), with Quaternal Logic | The track's clip is Live's clip of it, whose source is the constellation, sounding on the torus. | — | M |
| FC09 | [Lived-in] I have the same thing as a page in Central and a scene in Expressions, and work on one of them. | #709 → §16.5 · Clip view in each mode | Each mode's owner | Both clips name the same constellation in their Clip panels; the edit changes the clip I worked on. | An edit in one mode's clip changes neither the constellation nor the other mode's clip. | P |
| FC10 | [Lived-in] A passage I read in Central becomes the source of a task I commission in Factory. | §3 · Factory, Session view | Software Factory, with Central | The task keeps the passage as its source and where it came from. | The crossing launches nothing by itself. | P |
| FC11 | [Lived-in] A sweep of the field I made in Expressions becomes a stop on a walk in Technē. | §3 · Technē, the Journey | The shell (Technē), with Expressions | The walk's stop launches the scene it carries, and the scene keeps where it came from. | — | P |
| FC12 | [Lived-in] A palace I built in Technē goes back to Central's wiki. | §3 · Central | Central, with AIKit (the wiki) | It returns to the wiki as a proposal. | As a proposal it changes nothing in the wiki by itself. | P |

## CL — Read a thing through a frame or a lens without changing it

**Why:** "So really the focus needs to be making quaternal logic not an add-on, seeing how utterly essential it is" (UX chat 27 Oct). "no one needs to know QL for it to work" (#709).
**Where:** #709 → manual §10.3, §16.5, §16.7 · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25 (the Groove Pool, the Clip panel's Groove)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CL01 | [Lived-in] I want to see the ways of reading I can apply to my work. | #709 → §10.3, §16.7 · the pool (⌥⌘6) | Quaternal Logic | The pool under the browser holds QL frames and MEF lenses, the DLW's grooves. | The pool holds no device, no instrument and no pane's contents. | P |
| CL02 | [Lived-in] I take a frame or a lens out of a constellation to use on other work, as Live takes a groove out of a clip. | #709 → §16.7 · the pool | Quaternal Logic | The frame or lens stands in the pool, ready to apply. | The constellation it came from stays as it was. | P |
| CL03 | [Lived-in] I choose a frame or a lens for a clip and read the clip through it. | #709 → §16.5 · Clip view, Clip panel, where Live's Groove stands | Quaternal Logic, with the mode's owner | The clip reads through the frame or lens I chose. | Applying it never changes the clip's source. | P |
| CL04 | [Lived-in] I tune an MEF lens to read more finely. | #709 → §16.7 · the pool | Quaternal Logic | The lens's sublenses stand as its parameters, and turning them changes the reading. | The source stays as it is. | P |
| CL05 | [Lived-in] A reading holds, and I want it kept in my knowledge. | #709 → §16.7 · the pool, Commit | AIKit (the wiki), with Quaternal Logic | Commit writes the reading into the wiki. | Until I commit, nothing is written into the wiki. | P |
| CL06 | [Lived-in] In Live mode I want the set's own grooves beside the frames and lenses. | §16.7; #709 · the pool, the switch at its head | The shell (Live), with the set | Each of the set's grooves shows Groove, Base, Quantize, Timing, Random and Velocity, with Global Amount at the foot, read from the set and written into it. | Empty, the pool reads "Drop clips or grooves here". | M |

## FL — Keep my sets, files and media where they belong

**Why:** "Obviously, at the data level and the file level, and the UX level, the fluidity of moving the data, the information between these modes is also really important." (UX chat 27 Oct). "the wiki would hold the raw nature of the information … without a specific file needed, but it would link to the various modalities it's expressed through" (#709).
**Where:** #709 → manual §10.2, §10.3, §10.9, §18.1 · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25 (session model, the `.als`); Obsidian 1.7.7 (saving)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FL01 | [Arriving] I bring in a project and need its pages, scenes and sets to stay in their own formats. | #709; §10.11 Projects step · the browser, Work place | Central, with AIKit (the constellation as index) | The project is a folder and its constellation is its index. Each mode keeps its own format: Markdown and HTML pages, Expressions' JSON, real `.als` for Live. Capability tables and code are reached by their exact rows and lines in the same way. | Moving between modes transcribes; nothing is converted. No Markdown copy is made to suit a parser; a format no reader supports is named as such. | M |
| FL02 | [Lived-in] I save the set I am working in. | #709; §10.2 What is kept · the main area | The shell | The set is a DLW-native file in the project whose clips reference nodes; reopened, its tracks, clips, their order and their settings come back. | In Central, saving the set is the day's close. | M |
| FL03 | [Arriving] I open my Ableton set in Live mode, work in it and save. | #709 · Live, Session view and Arrangement view | The shell (Live), reading and writing through `packages/live-set` | Live reads the real `.als`, and saving writes a real `.als` that Ableton opens. | — | M |
| FL04 | [Lived-in] I record, and keep the recording as material to work from. | #709 · Arrangement view | The mode's owner | The recording is source material kept in the project; the set refers to it by its path inside the project. | — | M |
| FL05 | [Lived-in] I render a piece and later need to know what it came from. | #709 · the browser, the render | The mode's owner | The render names what it was made from and can be remade. | A render is never edited in place. | M |
| FL06 | [Lived-in] The source of a render has moved since I made it. | #709 · the render | The mode's owner | The render shows that its source has moved, and I remake it from the source as it is now. | — | M |
| FL07 | [Arriving] I import audio or another outside file into a project. | #709 · the browser | The mode's owner | The import keeps its origin. | — | M |
| FL08 | [Lived-in] I add large media to a project that is kept in git. | #709 · the project folder | Central | The media is held by content hash and stays out of git; the set still finds it by its path inside the project. | — | M |
| FL09 | [Lived-in] I want a page's file, or a world's folder, in my own editor or in Finder. | §10.9 · a clip's or a track's menu: Open, Reveal | Central finds the file or folder and asks the computer to open or show it | Open hands it to the application my computer opens it with; Reveal shows it in Finder. | Neither changes the world. | M |
| FL10 | [Lived-in] I save a page and need to know whether the save landed. | §18.1 Clip panel, Save state · Clip view | Central | Save state reads Saved, Unchanged, Stale, Conflict or Refused; a save makes the page's state a new revision. | A stale or conflicting save keeps my draft and shows what changed. | M |
| FL11 | [Lived-in] I reopen a set and need the window as I left it. | #709 → §10.2 · the main area, the rail, the detail area | The shell | The view in front, the panes and what they hold, the widgets on the rail and the sizes of each part come back with the set. | — | P |
| FL12 | [Lived-in] I start new work from an arrangement I use often. | #709 → §10.3 · the browser | The shell | A template in the browser gives the new set that arrangement. | — | P |

## CW — Bring the worlds I work on into today

**Where:** manual §5, §18.1 (The set, The track, The mixer row, The clip, Takes and Arrangement lanes, The Main track) · **Port sources:** Obsidian 1.7.7 (saving, graph and links); VS Code's workbench (the code-editor side)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CW01 | [Lived-in] I switch to Central and need today's work in front of me. | §18.1 · Pane view, Device view, the widget rail | Central | Central opens on today's Day and a Flow side by side in two panes, the world's chain in Device view, and the world's agent, the Packet and the Trail on the rail; the transport shows today's date. | — | P |
| CW02 | [Lived-in] I bring a world into today by dragging it from the browser onto an empty track. | §18.1 The track, §10.11 · the browser, Session view | Central | The world is a track in today's set, its pages are the track's clips, and it sends its work to the Day. | Dropping a folder on a track never sets it up; a folder becomes a world through Setup's Projects step. | H (core) |
| CW03 | [Lived-in] I open a page to pick it up where I left it. | §18.1 The clip · Session view, Clip view | Central, with each agent's session | The page plays. A Flow resumes with its people and agents back in their seats, each agent's session where it stopped; a Being or a Thing opens to be read. The NOWs widget lists it with its machine and the agent at work on it. | Opening a page changes nothing in it; only writing does. | P |
| CW04 | [Lived-in] I have finished with a page for now. | §18.1 The clip · the track's Stop, or the clip's square | Central | The moment closes and the page leaves what is open now. | What I stopped leaves tomorrow's grid and stays where it lay in today's Arrangement view. | P |
| CW05 | [Lived-in] I need to know which pages have unsaved work. | §18.1 The clip · Session view and Arrangement view | Central | Each clip shows Draft, Saved or Edited since save on the grid and on the ruler. | — | P |
| CW06 | [Lived-in] I open the pages of a morning's practice together. | §18.1 The set, The Main track · a row and its launcher on the Main track | Central | The row launcher opens every page in its row at once; I name the row as I like. | A row opens pages inside today; it brings no other world into the set. | P |
| CW07 | [Lived-in] I add or change a file in a world's folder from another editor. | §18.1 The mixer row (the input is the Folder) · Session view | Central | A file I add shows as a clip; a change made in any editor shows on its clip. | A world's pages stay in its folder as ordinary files; the track shows them and does not hold them. | M |
| CW08 | [Lived-in] I want to see what works on a world's pages. | §18.1, §16.2 · Device view, the world's chain | Central, with Actuation and Software Factory | The chain stands left to right: the Packet, the agent of the world, its Scope, then Docs. | — | P |
| CW09 | [Lived-in] I want to see the day as I lived it. | §18.1 Takes and Arrangement lanes · Arrangement view | Central | Each page lies along its world's track for as long as it played, and the Day's die lies on the Main track below them; nothing needed recording by hand. | — | P |
| CW10 | [Lived-in] I want a passage back from an earlier revision of a page. | §18.1 Takes and Arrangement lanes · Arrangement view, take lanes | Central | Selecting a range in an earlier revision and pressing Enter brings it back into the page; the draft shows as a take when it differs from the last save. | — | P |
| CW11 | [Lived-in] I want to meet a being or a thing in another instrument. | §18.1 Opening a page · the clip's menu, Open in | Central, with the instrument's owner | The world's reading view, the Journey at the page's stop, or the Sky at its placements opens in a pane. | A choice the page has nothing for stays closed and says why. | P |

## CM — Set how each world takes part in today

**Where:** manual §18.1 (The track, The mixer row), §16.1 (Live's acts, kept as Live keeps them) · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25 (session model)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CM01 | [Lived-in] I start a new world and want it to start with the right pages. | §18.1 The track, Kind · the track's head | Central | Project, Book, Practice or Other chooses the pages it starts with; a Practice starts with a journal and keeps itself to me. | — | P |
| CM02 | [Lived-in] I set where a world's pages are kept and where it stands on my planet. | §18.1 The track, Folder and Place · the track's head | Central, with the shell (Technē's World) | The world's pages are kept in the folder I chose; its place on my planet's surface is where I set it, and its body turns in my sky. | — | P |
| CM03 | [Lived-in] I keep a side project out of the Day. | §18.1 The mixer row, Output · the track's mixer row | Central | With Output set to None, the world's work does not reach the Day's faces. | Every world sends to the Day unless its Output is None. | P |
| CM04 | [Lived-in] I switch a world off for today without removing it. | §18.1 The mixer row, Activator · the track's mixer row | Central | Its pages do not open to play, and its track is dimmed, keeping its place and its settings. | — | P |

| CM06 | [Lived-in] I visit a world someone has let me into, and choose whether to follow its changes. | §18.1 The mixer row, Monitor · the track's mixer row | Central, with the other world's owner | In follows its changes as they happen, Off keeps it as I last saw it, Auto follows while one of its pages plays. | My own worlds have no Monitor. | P |

| CT03 | [Lived-in] I want my pages saved as I go, and to say when a save lands. | §10.5 Metronome, Quantize · the transport | Central | Metronome saves as I go; Quantize sets when a save lands. | — | M |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| CP02 | [Lived-in] I want the page as its template draws it, or its source text. | §18.1 Editor tabs · Clip view, Page and Source | Central | Page shows the page as its template draws it; Source shows its source text. | — | P |
| CP03 | [Lived-in] I mark the passages that matter as I read. | §18.1 Tools, Highlight · the selection tool | Central | The passage is marked in the colour I chose, and the highlight stays in the page. | Choosing Highlight again on a highlight removes it. | CP04 | [Lived-in] I want to highlight by dragging over the text. | §18.1 Highlight, Pen; §10.5 Draw · Clip view, the transport | Central | With the pen on, a drag over text is a highlight; Draw in the transport turns it on. | — | P |
| CP05 | [Lived-in] I skim a long entry by what I marked. | §18.1 Highlight, Show · Clip view | Central | Show set to Highlights only fades everything but the highlights. | — | P |
| CP06 | [Lived-in] I keep my own thought beside a passage. | §18.1 Notes · the selection tool, Clip view | Central | A note opens anchored to the selection, the entry or the document, and shows its anchor; Timing records whether I wrote it during or after reading; Show and Order set which notes appear and how. | A note is mine: an agent does not read it unless I add it to the packet. | P |
| CP07 | [Lived-in] I set how the words are set without changing them. | §18.1 Text · Clip view | Central | Style (Body, Head, Quote, List) and Emphasis are kept in the page. | Text never changes a word. | P |
| CP08 | [Lived-in] I read a long page with less strain. | §18.1 Text, Size · Clip view | Central | The type shows at 13 to 22 pt. | Size changes only my view, never the page. | P |

| CP11 | [Lived-in] I colour a page's clip so two readings stay apart. | §18.1 Clip panel, Colour · Clip view | Central | The clip shows its colour on the grid and the ruler. | A colour has only the meaning I give it. | P |

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CJ01 | [Lived-in] I read an exchange entry by entry. | §18.1 Editor tabs, Dialogue · Clip view | Central | The entries stand in order, each with its author; an agent entry shows the agent's name and, when known, its session. | Switching tabs reads the same entries; it makes no second source. | P |
| CJ02 | [Lived-in] Before a meeting, I find the threads nobody has answered. | §18.1 Editor tabs, Flow · Clip view | Central | The same entries stand as threads with the relations drawn between them. | — | P |
| CJ03 | [Lived-in] I keep a thinking journal, one page a day. | §18.1 Editor tabs, Journal; Journal page; Speak as · Clip view | Central | One person's page at a time, earlier pages listed below; Journal page chooses the date I read. | — | P |
| CJ04 | [Lived-in] I write the next entry in someone's name and say how it connects. | §18.1 Speak as, Relation · the head of the editor | Central | The entry belongs to the participant I chose, as a Reply, a Branch, a Converge, a Source or an Artifact of the entry I selected. | — | P |
| CJ05 | [Lived-in] An earlier entry, mine or an agent's, said something wrong. | §18.1 Relation: Correct · Clip view | Central | A new entry with the relation Correct says what the earlier one should have said. | The earlier entry stays on the page. | P |
| CJ06 | [Lived-in] I add a person or an agent to a Flow. | §18.1 Participants; §19.24 · Clip panel | Central | The participant is named with an initial, a person drawn solid, an agent as points, an agent tied to a session where it has one. | Adding a participant starts nothing and is no proof of who wrote. Nor does it make the participants a group: a group is made with the Agentset rack. | P |
| CJ07 | [Lived-in] I attach a photograph, a recording or a clip to an entry. | §18.1 Media · the head of the editor | Central | Image, video or audio files up to 12 MB each are kept inside the page. | Media stays inside the page and never leaves my ground. | M |
| CJ08 | [Lived-in] I want an agent to read a whole Flow and tell me what it finds there. | §18.1 Contemplate, §17.8 The Thought page · Pane view, the Document's Thought page | Central, with the agent | Preflight shows what Contemplate would read and touch; holding Contemplate runs it once under that preflight, and what it returns waits for me. A week later, Changed since shows where the Flow has gone. | Contemplate never runs by itself. If anything changed after the preflight it refuses and says so. | P |
| CJ09 | [Lived-in] I ask two agents in a Flow the same question, and each answers on its own. | §18.1 A Flow, Participants · Clip view | Central (the Flow), with AIKit carrying each ask | Each answer arrives as its own entry, with its author and what it read, and a later entry can draw both together. | One agent failing leaves the other's answer standing; no answer is taken from whichever reply came last. | P |
| CJ10 | [Lived-in] I address an entry to one participant while everyone in the Flow can read it. | §18.1 A Flow, Speak as · Clip view | Central (the Flow), with AIKit | Only the one I addressed is asked to answer; the others read it without being woken, and the Flow shows who was asked and who reads. | A private aside goes into a narrower thread of its own, never hidden in the shared page. A reply to the exact agent that answered reaches that agent, never a newer one under the same name. | P |
| CJ11 | [Lived-in] I close the Flow while an answer is still coming, and come back later. | §18.1 A Flow · Clip view | Central (the Flow), with AIKit | The answer is in the Flow once, against the question as it stood when I asked. | If I changed the question meanwhile, the answer shows the difference. If the system cannot tell whether the ask went out, it asks me before sending again. Closing my Day never resets the discussion or its pending answers. | M |
| CJ12 | [Lived-in] I let a chosen group of agents work a question among themselves within limits I set. | §18.1 A Flow, Participants; §19.24 · Clip view; Messages | Central (the Flow); AIKit, with the group's type | They answer one another within its purpose, its members and its turns and time, and stop or wait when the question is answered or the budget is spent. | No agent answers itself or starts a loop of replies. Cancelling stops what is not yet sent and keeps what already happened. | P |
| CJ13 | [Arriving] I bring someone new into a Flow partway. | §18.1 Participants · Clip panel | Central (the Flow) | They receive the part of the history I chose, and the Flow shows what actually reached them. | Bringing them in shows them nothing else of my world, and joining starts no agent until I ask. | P |

## CB — Tie a page to what it is about and where it stands

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CB01 | [Lived-in] I make a page about a person, an agent or an object. | §18.1 Subject, Kind of being · Clip panel | Central | A Being page holds a person or an agent; a Thing holds an object; each has a title, an introduction, an opening, numbered sections and an In relation list. | — | P |
| CB02 | [Lived-in] I keep a claim on a page honest. | §18.1 Sources · Clip panel | Central | Each source stands at the revision I read. | — | P |
| CB03 | [Lived-in] I set where a being stands in my world, and go and see it there. | §18.1 Place · Clip panel; the world instrument | Central, with the shell (Technē's World) | The page stands at its place in my world, and the world instrument shows it there. | An empty Place stays empty; nothing guesses a place. | P |
| CB04 | [Lived-in] I tie a letter to the town it was posted from. | §2, §18.1 Anchor · Clip panel | Central | The page is anchored to a real place on Earth. | Anchoring changes nothing on Earth. | P |
| CB05 | [Lived-in] I want an object shown as a form, not only as words. | §18.1 Expression · Clip panel | Central, with Expressions | The page shows the Expression I set. | — | P |
| CB06 | [Lived-in] I say which subject offices a page takes up. | §18.1 Office · Clip panel | Central | One or more of 1 to 4 stand on the page. | — | P |
| CB07 | [Lived-in] I set how a page is composed and read it at night. | §18.1 Layout, Tone · Clip panel | Central | Portrait or Editorial; Paper or Ink. | — | P |
| CB08 | [Lived-in] I read how a page relates to others, and on whose word. | §18.1 Rules · the In relation side list | Central | Each relation shows its standing (fact, claim, inferred or hypothesis) and who stated it. | — | P |
| CB09 | [Lived-in] I open a Being page of one of my agents. | §18.1 Rules · Clip view | Central, reading the roster | The page reads the agent's card from my roster, as the Agent device does. | The page keeps no copy of the card. | P |

## CA — Hand a passage to an agent and keep only what helps

**Why:** "it kind of puts the AI tools next to human creativity tools in a way, which kind of gives, for me at least, gives everything the right place to land and the right emphasis" (UX chat 27 Oct).
**Where:** manual §5, §16.1, §18.1 (Tools, On the rail, Rules), §18.3, §19.14 · **Port sources:** Obsidian 1.7.7 (saving); VS Code's workbench (the code-editor side)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CA01 | [Lived-in] I put exactly the passages an agent needs in front of it. | §18.1 To packet, §18.3 · the selection tool, Capture in the transport; the Packet | Central (Packet) | Each item shows where it came from, a line of its text and its size; the total size stays in view on the device, the widget and in the conversation alike. | Nothing enters the packet unless I add it; a highlight or a note enters only with To packet. If the page changes under a passage after I add it, the packet marks the passage and nothing is sent until I look again; a send that fails leaves the packet as it was. | P |
| CA02 | [Lived-in] I read the whole packet before sending it, and take out what the agent does not need. | §18.3 Full · the Packet full in a pane | Central (Packet) | Every item in full with its source, its size and a mark to remove it, the total at the foot. | — | P |
| CA03 | [Lived-in] I want To packet to take a whole entry or the whole document, not the selection. | §18.3 Add · the Packet | Central (Packet) | To packet takes the Selection, the Entry or the Document. | — | P |
| CA04 | [Lived-in] I keep the cost of an answer down. | §18.3 Limit · the Packet | Central (Packet) | The packet stops at the size I set. | An item that does not fit waits for my decision. | P |
| CA05 | [Lived-in] I want to ask again with the same material. | §18.3 After sending · the Packet | Central (Packet) | With After sending on Keep the packet stays full after it is sent; on Clear it empties. | — | P |
| CA06 | [Lived-in] I ask the agent about the passage I selected and want its answer in the page. | §5, §18.1 Answer this, §18.3 · Play in the transport, or Answer this in the selection tool | Actuation (the agent of the world), with Central | The answer arrives as a new entry with its sources, and showing the packet it came from; a source followed back lands on the line it came from. | The agent reads the packet and the places Scope allows and nothing else of mine. Its entry lands as Scope and Arm allow: as a proposal waiting on the page, or directly with a receipt. | H (core) |
| CA07 | [Lived-in] I decide whether an answer adds to the page or replaces what I selected. | §5, §18.1 Answer this; §10.5 Overdub · the transport | Central | Overdub joins the answer as a new entry, or replaces the selected passage. | — | P |
| CA08 | [Lived-in] An agent wants to change my page while its world is not armed. | §5, §16.1, §18.1 The Main track, Rules; §19.14 · Approvals on the Main track and its tile on the rail; the page | Software Factory (Approvals), with Central | The change waits as a proposal in Approvals and on the page; I answer Approve, Changes or Deny, and an approved change lands with a receipt. | Nothing lands until I answer. | P |
| CA09 | [Lived-in] I trust a world's agent to write directly for a while. | §5, §18.1 The mixer row, Arm; §10.5 Arm · the track's mixer row, the transport | Central, with Actuation | The agent's edits land in the world's pages directly, each with a receipt. | With Arm off, every agent write arrives as a proposal. | P |
| CA10 | [Lived-in] I changed a passage the agent wrote, and want to hand it back. | §5, §10.5 Re-enable · the page, the transport | Central, with Actuation | A light comes on when I change the agent's passage; one press hands it back. | — | P |
| CA11 | [Lived-in] I keep a line for my own ground without deciding yet what it is. | §18.1 Remember · the selection tool | Central | The passage is kept word for word with where it came from, as a proposal in my ground, or in the project's ground for a project's page. | It stays unrecognised until I recognise it in Docs. | P |
| CA12 | [Lived-in] I want the packet's contents outside the DLW. | §18.3 Rules · the Packet | Central (Packet) | The packet copies out as data. | Copying sends nothing. | P |
| CA13 | [Lived-in] I talk to the agent of the world I have selected without leaving the page. | §18.1 On the rail · the Agent tile on the rail | Actuation, with Central | The tile shows the agent's face, its state, the last lines of its thread and a composer. | — | P |
