**Where:** manual §9, §21.1 Clip view, §16.5 · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25: session model, object model

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LN01 | [Lived-in] I select a MIDI clip and edit its notes in a roll whose key column is my playing surface. | §21.1 Clip view, Editor tabs · Clip view, Notes tab | Shell (Live): the clip | The notes stand on the Jankó key column in its layout and colours, with a lane below for velocity or chance | When the set's owner cannot be reached, the clip keeps its last content, dimmed, and cannot be edited (§16.5) | M |
| LN02 | [Lived-in] I draw notes into a clip, and record into it by playing while it is open. | §21.1 Editor tabs, §10.5 Draw · Clip view, Notes tab · Draw; the playing surface | Shell (Live): the clip | Drawn and played notes appear in the roll and play the Torus | An edit that fails stays in the editor, and one line in the status bar says what failed (§16.5) | M |
| LN03 | [Lived-in] I set where a clip starts and ends, whether it loops, and its own metre. | §21.1 The Clip panel · Clip view, Clip panel · Start and End, Loop, Position and Length (each with Set), Signature | Shell (Live): the clip | The clip plays and loops between the points I set; Set takes the playhead's place | A clip on the ruler keeps its own loop and metre wherever it is moved (§16.6) | P |
| LN04 | [Lived-in] I double a clip's loop and its notes. | §21.1 The Clip panel · Clip view · Duplicate | Shell (Live): the clip | The loop is twice as long, with its notes copied into the new half | MIDI clips only | M |
| LN05 | [Lived-in] I rewrite a clip's notes with the pitch and time tools. | §21.1 Tools (Pitch and time) · Clip view, Tools · Invert, Add Interval, Stretch ×2 and /2, Grid and Set Length, Humanize, Reverse, Legato | Shell (Live): the clip | The notes are turned over, thickened by an interval in scale degrees, stretched, evened, loosened, reversed or joined | Undo takes each change back as one named step (§10.9) | M |
| LN06 | [Lived-in] I transform a clip: arpeggiate, connect, ornament, quantize, recombine, set its span, strum or bend its timing. | §21.1 Tools (Transform) · Clip view, Tools · a tool, its settings, Transform | Shell (Live): the clip | The tool reshapes what is there when I press Transform, or at once while its auto-apply is on | A transform reshapes the content as a whole; a device on the chain would change the sound and leave the clip as it is (§16.5) | M |
| LN07 | [Lived-in] I generate new notes: a rhythm, random notes in a range, a melody that follows a drawn shape, or chords. | §21.1 Tools (Generate) · Clip view, Tools · Rhythm, Seed, Shape, Stacks, Generate | Shell (Live): the clip | The generator writes notes into the clip when I press Generate | Undo removes what it wrote as one named step (§10.9) | M |
| LN08 | [Lived-in] I shape each note's own expression. | §21.1 Editor tabs · Clip view, MPE tab | Shell (Live): the clip | Each note's pitch is drawn on it, with lanes for slide, pressure, velocity and release velocity; pressure from the surface lands here | MPE belongs to MIDI clips; an audio clip shows its Sample tab instead (§21.1) | M |
| LN09 | [Lived-in] I draw an envelope that moves a parameter over the clip's own time. | §21.1 Envelopes · Clip view, Envelopes tab · Device, Control, Mode, Linked | Shell (Live): the clip | Automation sets the value over the clip; Modulation moves it against the part's own value; Linked follows the clip's loop, Unlinked has its own | The sky is a modulator, not an envelope: it shows on the parameter it moves (§21.1) | M |
| LN10 | [Lived-in] I fit an audio clip to the set's tempo. | §21.1 Tools (Audio utilities), Editor tabs · Clip view, Sample tab · Warp, Warp mode, BPM, /2, ×2 | Shell (Live): the clip | The waveform shows its transients and warp markers, and the sample plays in time | The warp mode decides how the sample is stretched: Beats, Tones, Texture, Re-Pitch, Complex or Complex Pro | M |
| LN11 | [Lived-in] I reverse, fade, level and transpose an audio clip. | §21.1 Tools (Audio utilities) · Clip view · Reverse, Fade, RAM, HiQ, Gain, Pitch | Shell (Live): the clip | The sample plays backwards, with short edge fades, at the gain and pitch I set | HiQ resamples at high quality only when the sample is transposed | M |
| LN12 | [Lived-in] I move an audio clip's transients onto the grid. | §21.1 Tools (Quantize) · Clip view · Grid, Amount, Apply | Shell (Live): the clip | The transients move toward the grid by the amount I set | Undo takes the move back as one step (§10.9) | M |
| LN13 | [Lived-in] I draw envelopes of an audio clip's own controls. | §21.1 Envelopes · Clip view, Envelopes tab | Shell (Live): the clip | Gain, Transposition, Sample Offset and the warp mode's controls move over the clip | These envelopes have no Automation or Modulation switch | M |
| LN14 | [Lived-in] I give Clip view the whole main area while I edit. | §10.2, §10.8 · Clip view · ⌥⌘E | Shell | Clip view fills the main area; pressing again brings back the view I left as it was | Only Clip view can take the main area from the three views (§10.2) | P |
| LN15 | [Lived-in] I rename, recolour and switch off a clip, then undo the rename. | §10.9, §21.1 The Clip panel · Clip view, the clip's menu · ⌘R, the palette, 0, ⌘Z | Shell (Live): the clip | The change shows on the grid and the ruler; Undo names its step, such as "Rename" with the clip's name | An act the owner refuses shows its reason on the line under the grid (§10.9) | P |

## LR — Record, comp and arrange

**Why:** "recordings are source material" (#709)
**Where:** manual §9, §21.1 Takes and Arrangement lanes, §21.2 Transport, §16.6, §16.8; #709 Media · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25: session model

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LR01 | [Lived-in] I arm a part and record what I play into a clip in Session view. | §21.1 The mixer row, §10.9 Empty slot · Session view · Arm, a slot's record button, Record | Shell (Live): the set | A new clip holds the notes I played, marked with the record light while recording | Arm waits in the track's menu, since its Live key plays a note (§10.9) | M |
| LR02 | [Lived-in] I record into Arrangement view as notes, then add more over what is there. | §21.2 Transport · Arrangement view · Record, Overdub | Shell (Live): the set | What I play lies along the ruler as notes; Overdub adds notes without replacing them | Punch in and out limit where recording may happen (LR10) | M |
| LR03 | [Lived-in] I keep a phrase I just played without having pressed Record. | §9, §21.2 Transport · the transport · Capture | Shell (Live): the set | The phrase becomes a clip I can edit and arrange | Undo takes the capture back as one named step (§10.9) | M |
| LR04 | [Lived-in] I record several passes of a phrase and comp the best parts into one. | §21.1 Takes and Arrangement lanes, §16.6 · Arrangement view, take lanes (⌥⌘U) · Enter | Shell (Live): the set | Each pass lies as a take lane under the part; a range selected in a take and Enter bring it into the main lane, and the take lane marks the range it gave | The main lane shows which take each stretch came from, and the comp is one undo step (§16.6) | H (core) |
| LR05 | [Lived-in] I record a parameter's moves as an automation lane. | §21.1, §16.8 · Arrangement view, automation lanes · Arm, then move a parameter | Shell (Live): the set | A lane under the part shows the moves, its head naming the device and parameter | A value I take over gives control back only when I press Re-enable | M |
| LR06 | [Lived-in] I draw automation over the clips. | §16.6 Arrangement view, §10.8 · Arrangement view · A (Automation Mode), + and − on a lane | Shell (Live): the set | The head gains a device and a parameter chooser and I draw the moves over the clips; + opens a lane of its own | − hides a lane and keeps its moves | M |
| LR07 | [Lived-in] I record what I launch in Session view into the arrangement. | §16.8 · Session view, Arrangement view · Record while launching clips | Shell (Live): the set | What played lies in Arrangement view as clips, with takes under them | Back to Arrangement lights while the Session differs from the ruler (§21.1) | M |
| LR08 | [Lived-in] I arrange the piece on the ruler: move, trim, roll, slip, slide and split clips. | §16.6 Arrangement view, §10.8 · Arrangement view · Move mode, Trim mode, ⌘E | Shell (Live): the set | Each clip keeps what it is and changes only where it stands in time; a split's two lengths add up to the original | Every edit is one labelled undo step; Snap pulls edges to clips, markers and the playhead, and Alt turns it off while I drag | M |
| LR09 | [Lived-in] I consolidate several clips into one, and crop a clip to its loop. | §10.8, §10.9 · Arrangement view · ⌘J, ⇧⌘J | Shell (Live): the set | The stretch becomes one clip, or the clip is cut to what it plays | Each is one undo step (§16.6 Rules) | M |
| LR10 | [Lived-in] I set the loop brace and punch in and out, so the transport repeats a stretch and records only inside it. | §10.1 Loop, §16.6 · Arrangement view · loop brace, Punch in, Loop, Punch out | Shell (Live): the set | The transport plays and repeats only between the in and out points; recording lands only inside the punch | Punch marks a place on the ruler, never a clock time (§16.1) | M |
| LR11 | [Lived-in] I mark points and name sections along the ruler. | §16.6 Arrangement view · Arrangement view · markers, named sections | Shell (Live): the set | Named, coloured points and sections run along the top of the ruler | Snap pulls the edges I drag to markers (§16.6) | P |
| LR12 | [Arriving] I arm an audio part and record my voice from my audio interface. | §21.1 The input; #709 Media · Session or Arrangement view · Arm, Record | Shell (Live): the set; the recording is kept in the project | The take lands as an audio clip; its file is source material inside the project, which the set refers to by path | Large media stays out of git, held by content hash (#709) | M |

## LF — Bring in, keep and render the set's files

**Why:** "real `.als` read and written by `packages/live-set` for Ableton" · "renders are derived (each names what it was made from, can be remade, shows when its source has moved, is never edited in place)" (#709)
**Where:** manual §10.3, §16.7, §10.2 What is kept, §21.1; #709 Files, Media and The pool; #708 · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25: session model, object model

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LF01 | [Arriving] I open my existing Ableton set from Live's browser and find my parts and clips there. | §10.3 (Live leads with your sets), §16.7 Open · the browser · a set | Shell (Live): the set, read by `packages/live-set` (#709) | The set's parts, clips, notes and tempo stand in Session view and Arrangement view, ready to play on the Torus | What cannot be read stays as it was last read, and one line in the status bar says what failed (§10.2) | M |
| LF02 | [Arriving] I preview a set before I open it. | §10.3, §16.7 Preview · the browser, its foot | Shell (Live) | The preview shows at the foot of the list, where Live plays a sample I select | A preview changes nothing (§10.3) | P |
| LF03 | [Lived-in] I save the set and come back to it later. | §10.2 What is kept; #709 Files · the set | Shell (Live): the set, a DLW-native file whose clips reference nodes | Reopening brings back its parts, clips, their order and settings | How I looked at it is kept by the workspace, not the set (§10.2) | M |
| LF04 | [Lived-in] I write the set as a real `.als` and open it in Ableton Live. | #709 Files · the set | Shell (Live): `packages/live-set` writes the `.als` | Ableton Live opens the file with the same parts, clips and notes | Each mode keeps its own format; the `.als` is Live's, beside the DLW-native set (#709) | M |
| LF05 | [Yours] I relaunch the DLW and Live opens my own world's set. | #708; §10.11 Ground · Live mode | Shell (Live): state keyed to the world folder I chose and named | My own set opens, not a copy left behind by a test or remembered from another place | A test never leaves a copy behind as remembered state (#708) | M |
| LF06 | [Arriving] I bring a sound file of my own into a part. | §10.3 Material, §16.7 Bring into the set; #709 Media · the browser · drag onto an audio part | Shell (Live): the set; the import keeps its origin | The sound plays as an audio clip and still names where it came from | The set refers to media by paths inside the project (#709) | M |
| LF07 | [Lived-in] I render the piece to an audio file. | #709 Media · the whole piece, as the Main track carries it (§21.1) | Shell (Live): a render, derived from the set | The file names the set it was made from and can be remade from it | A render is never edited in place (#709) | M |
| LF08 | [Lived-in] I find a render whose source has moved and remake it. | #709 Media · the browser | Shell (Live): the render | The render shows that its source has moved; remade, it matches the source again | Until it is remade, the render keeps naming what it was made from (#709) | M |
| LF09 | [Lived-in] I keep my working layout with the set so it returns when the set opens. | #709 The pool; §10.2 layout · the main area, the rail, the detail area | Shell (Live): the set keeps its workspace's layout | Opening the set brings back the view in front, the panes, the widgets on the rail and the sizes I left | Launching a workspace changes no page, no world and no Day (§16.7 Rules) | P |
| LF10 | [Lived-in] I keep an arrangement I reuse as a template and start a new set from it. | #709 The pool · the browser | Shell (Live): templates in the browser | The template stands in the browser, and a new set starts from it with its parts and layout | Reusable arrangements stand in the browser as templates, not in the pool (#709) | P |

## LG — Sound a constellation, and play it through grooves

**Why:** "the wiki constellations are what persist, the modes make the data of the constellations visible in their different modes" · "you don't have to do anything other than transcribe, which is just shifting to something implicit, not recreating or reprocessing." (#709)
**Where:** manual §3, §10.3, §16.5, §16.7, §21.1 The Clip panel (Groove); #709 The clip's source, The pool · **Port sources:** Ableton Live 12.0.25: session model (the Groove Pool)

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LG01 | [Lived-in] I select a constellation in the browser while in Live and hear what Live makes of it. | §10.3 Preview; #709 The clip's source · the browser, its foot | AIKit: the wiki constellation; Shell (Live): the preview | The preview sounds Live's reading of the constellation | A preview changes nothing (§10.3) | M |
| LG02 | [Lived-in] I drop a constellation onto an empty Live track and get Live's clip of it. | §16.7 Bring into the set; #709 · Session view | Shell (Live): the set; AIKit: the constellation | A clip stands on the track, and its Clip panel names the constellation as its source, as a sample is an audio clip's | A node needs no file: its files are its expressions, and the clip refers to the node (#709) | M |
| LG03 | [Lived-in] From a clip I show its constellation in the browser and keep it there as I change mode. | #709 The clip's source · Clip view (Clip panel) · Show in Browser | Shell: the browser; AIKit: the constellation | The browser goes to the constellation; in Expressions it is still selected there, and its preview shows what Expressions makes of it | Changing mode keeps what I selected and closes nothing (§3) | P |
| LG04 | [Lived-in] I change from Live to Central with that clip selected and find the same constellation written about. | §3; #709 · the transport · Mode | Shell: the modes; AIKit: the constellation | Transcribing is automatic: my selection stays and nothing is converted or reprocessed | A crossing never sends, shows or launches anything by itself (§3) | P |
| LG05 | [Lived-in] I try grooves on a clip and commit the one I keep into its notes. | §21.1 The Clip panel · Clip view · Groove, Hot-swap, Commit | Shell (Live): the set's grooves | Hot-swap tries another groove as the clip plays; Commit writes the groove into the notes | Choosing None takes the groove off the clip | M |
| LG06 | [Lived-in] I drop a clip on the pool to take its groove. | §10.3, §16.7 The pool · the pool | Shell (Live): the set's grooves | The clip gives up its groove, which stands in the pool for any clip | An empty pool reads "Drop clips or grooves here" | M |
| LG07 | [Lived-in] I set how strongly each groove acts, and all of them together. | §16.7 The pool · the pool (the switch at its head shows the set's grooves) · Groove, Base, Quantize, Timing, Random, Velocity; Global Amount | Shell (Live): the set's grooves | Every clip using a groove plays with the new amounts | Each groove is the set's, read from it and written into it (§16.7) | M |
| LG08 | [Lived-in] I play a clip through a QL frame or an MEF lens chosen from the pool. | #709 The pool · Clip view (Groove) and the pool | Shell (Live); Quaternal Logic: the frame or lens | The clip plays through the lens without its source changing; the lens's sublenses stand as its parameters | Live mode keeps Live's own grooves beside the frames and lenses (#709) | M |
| LG09 | [Lived-in] I commit a lens's reading into the wiki. | #709 The pool · Clip view · Commit | AIKit: the constellation's wiki takes the reading | The reading is kept with the constellation, for every mode to find | The source changes only when I commit (#709) | P |

## LS — See the sky over my world

**Why:** "Uranus: yes — ten voices." · "How the birth sky meets today's sky is defined by the computational systems." (#716)
**Where:** manual §2, §22.1 · **Port sources:** QL's own system; Ableton Live 12.0.25: device decodes, for the device's grammar

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LS01 | [Bare] I open the Sky tile on the rail and read where each body stands. | §22.1 Widget, To use · the rail · Sky tile | Quaternal Logic: the Sky (M2′) | Each of the ten bodies shows with its degree, and the tile stays on the rail in every mode | The Sky computes no astronomy in the interface: the sky arrives from its owner, and a body with no value for the date shows as missing | P |
| LS02 | [Bare] I open the Sky full in a pane and see the planets round the Earth. | §22.1 Full · Pane view · Sky (Full) | Quaternal Logic: the Sky | The Earth stands at the centre and the ten bodies on the ring at their degrees; a line under the date reads the day's planet and the hour's | The Earth is the anchor, not an eleventh planet | P |
| LS03 | [Lived-in] I set the sky to a day I cannot account for and see where everything stood. | §22.1 Parameters, Transport · Sky (Compact or Whole) · Date; Position | Quaternal Logic: the Sky | The ring moves to that day, and Position shows the date and time the sky stands at | The sky is calculated, not observed, and is not a second clock (§22.1, §22.3 Rules) | P |
| LS04 | [Lived-in] I let the sky run and watch the planets move. | §22.1 Parameters, Full · Sky · Day clock: Run, Run speed | Quaternal Logic: the Sky | The bodies move at 1 second per second up to 1 day per second | Hold keeps the date where I set it | P |
| LS05 | [Lived-in] I come back to now with one tap. | §22.1 Transport, Widget · the transport's Tap, or the Sky tile | Quaternal Logic: the Sky | The date returns to now | Position shows the date and time the sky stands at (§22.1 Transport) | P |
| LS06 | [Lived-in] I choose which bodies show and draw the angles between them. | §22.1 Parameters · Sky (Whole, Bodies and Overlay) · Bodies, Aspects | Quaternal Logic: the Sky | Only the bodies I keep on are drawn; Aspects draws the angles between them | If Quaternal Logic cannot be reached, the device keeps its last picture, dimmed, and its light is dark (§16.5) | P |
| LS07 | [Lived-in] I switch the Observer to Private so the hours are counted at my place. | §22.1 The day and the hour, Parameters · Sky · Observer | Quaternal Logic: the Sky | The day's and the hour's rulers are counted at my place | With no place, the Sky reads no rulers; a shared view never shows a private observer's place | P |
| LS08 | [Lived-in] I read whose hour it is. | §22.1 The day and the hour · Sky (Full; Whole, beside the groups) · the marked planet | Quaternal Logic: the Sky | The hour's planet is marked on the ring; the day's planet rules the first hour from sunrise, and the hours follow Saturn, Jupiter, Mars, Sun, Venus, Mercury, Moon | Twelve hours run from sunrise to sunset and twelve back, so a summer day hour is longer than a winter one | P |
| LS09 | [Bare] Having given Nara my birth data in Setup, I draw my birth sky round the sky of the date. | §22.1 Parameters · Sky · Natal ring | Quaternal Logic (Nara): my identity's birth sky | My birth ring draws round today's sky, for me alone | It stays mine until I share it; how the two skies meet is defined by the computational systems (#716) | P |
| LS10 | [Lived-in] I share my sky with a person of this world. | §22.1 Sharing your sky · Sky, Share page · Share with, Share | Quaternal Logic (Nara): the consent, kept with my identity | They can open my ring over the sky of the date and meet my presence in the world; the page lists whom I share with and since when | Nothing of my identity is copied or changed: sharing is a consent | P |
| LS11 | [Lived-in] I withdraw a consent I gave. | §22.1 Sharing your sky · Share page · Withdraw | Quaternal Logic (Nara): the consent | Their access ends and my ring is closed to them | Another person's ring opens only while their consent permits it | P |
| LS12 | [Lived-in] I open the birth sky of someone who shares theirs with me. | §22.1 Sharing your sky · Share page · Whose | Quaternal Logic (Nara): their consent | Their birth sky rings the sky of the date, as mine does | A consent that no longer permits it leaves their row dark and their ring closed | P |
| LS13 | [Lived-in] I read which parameters each body moves now. | §22.1 Parameters, Whole · Sky (Whole) · Moves | Quaternal Logic: the Sky | Below the groups, each body lists the parameters it moves | The Sky gives sources and does not decide what a reading means (§22.1 Rules) | P |

## LP — Let the planets move the sound

**Why:** "It's the wager that there is a symbolic mathematical layer to reality as such" (UX chat 27 Oct)
**Where:** manual §9, §22.2, §21.5, §10.4 · **Port sources:** QL's own system; Ableton Live 12.0.25: device decodes

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LP01 | [Lived-in] I give the Filter's cutoff to Venus so the sound opens and closes with the sky. | §22.2 To use, §21.5 Tips · Device view, a part's chain · Sky modulation: Add, Planet, Target, Amount | Quaternal Logic: Sky modulation (M2′) moves the target; the sky arrives from the Sky | The cutoff moves with Venus; its control names Venus and shows the base value and the value now | The sky reaches the sound as a plan made before I play, so no note is delayed; a planet with no value for the date shows as missing and is not guessed | H (core) |
| LP02 | [Lived-in] I choose whether a planet moves its target by its place in the sky or by its own interval. | §22.2 Parameters · Sky modulation (Whole, Assignments) · Use | Quaternal Logic: Sky modulation | Position maps the planet's place to the target's range; Own interval moves a frequency by the planet's just interval | Uranus has no interval, so it moves its target by position only | M |
| LP03 | [Lived-in] I set how far each planet moves its target, or reverse it. | §22.2 Parameters, Compact · Sky modulation (Compact: four macros named after the first four planets) · Amount | Quaternal Logic: Sky modulation | Each macro takes its planet's name and sets that assignment's amount; a negative amount reverses the move | Up to 16 assignments; the compact view reaches the first four | M |
| LP04 | [Lived-in] I give each note its own colour from the sky. | §22.2 Parameters, Tips · Sky modulation · Scope: Per note | Quaternal Logic: Sky modulation | Each note is moved separately; Global moves the whole sound | A parameter the sky moves names the planet on its control (§22.2 Rules) | M |
| LP05 | [Lived-in] I make the sky's pull a slow drift. | §22.2 Parameters, Tips · Sky modulation · Smoothing | Quaternal Logic: Sky modulation | The target follows the planet over up to 10 s | The device changes an assignment at once, and I can undo it (§22.2 Rules) | M |
| LP06 | [Lived-in] I set the modulation to a day I remember and listen to that sky. | §22.2 To use, Parameters · Sky modulation · Date | Quaternal Logic: Sky modulation | The part sounds as that day's sky moves it | Tap returns the date to now (§22.2 Transport) | M |
| LP07 | [Lived-in] I let the sky move the sound as seen from my own place. | §22.2 Parameters · Sky modulation · Observer: Private | Quaternal Logic: Sky modulation | My own place's sky moves the targets | Private needs a saved place | M |
| LP08 | [Lived-in] I take hold of a parameter the sky is moving, then give it back. | §22.2 Rules, §10.4 · the moved control · the hand-back light | Quaternal Logic: Sky modulation; Shell: the control | Moving it takes it over and the hand-back light comes on; pressing the light returns it to the planet | Re-enable gives a parameter back to its lane, or to the sky that moves it (§21.5 Transport) | M |
| LP09 | [Lived-in] I clear or change an assignment. | §22.2 Rules, Tips · Sky modulation (Whole, Assignments) | Quaternal Logic: Sky modulation | The change takes effect at once | I can undo it | M |
| LP10 | [Lived-in] I open Sky modulation full to see what each planet moves. | §22.2 Full · Pane view · Sky modulation (Full) | Quaternal Logic: Sky modulation | The sky ring fills the pane with a line from each planet to the parameter it moves, and the lines move as I play | If Quaternal Logic cannot be reached, the device keeps its last picture, dimmed, and its light is dark (§16.5) | P |
| LP11 | [Lived-in] I click a planet on the Sky modulation tile to go to its assignment. | §22.2 Widget · the rail · Sky modulation tile | Quaternal Logic: Sky modulation | The planets show with their degrees; a click opens that planet's assignment in Device view | If Quaternal Logic cannot be reached, the device keeps its last picture, dimmed, and its light is dark (§16.5) | P |
| LP12 | [Lived-in] I compare two skies by recording a phrase under each. | §22.2 Tips; §21.1 Takes · Sky modulation · Date; Record | Quaternal Logic: Sky modulation; Shell (Live): the set | The two takes sound the same phrase under two skies, side by side on the ruler | Each pass lies in its own take lane under the part (§21.1) | M |

## LK — Read the wheel of the clock and hear how it is cut

**Why:** "The eighteen apertures sit over the M3 clock, as in the Bimba map." (#716) · "the coordinate system, which includes the BIMba map and all the rest of it, that also needs to be made utterly central" (UX chat 27 Oct)
**Where:** manual §22.3, §22.7, §21.2 What strikes the Torus, §16.16; #716 (the Resonator bank joins Live's list in §9) · **Port sources:** QL's own system; Ableton Live 12.0.25: racks, device decodes

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LK01 | [Bare] I open the M3 clock and read where a moment stands on the wheel. | §22.3 To use, What the wheel holds · Pane view or a reading's chain · M3 clock (Full): Position | Quaternal Logic: the M3 clock (M3′) | The needle marks the degree; the four directions, the 24 governors and the 9 chambers show, and the governor names its hexagram and codon | A degree takes its governor from its quadrant (§22.3 Rules) | P |
| LK02 | [Lived-in] I read what a hexagram yields as a generation cell. | §22.3 What a hexagram yields · M3 clock (Whole, Position) · Readout: Generation | Quaternal Logic: the M3 clock | The cell shows its two pairs, its positive and negative codons, and whether it is non-dual | No matrix has a gap: every hexagram has a cell in each of the three (§22.3 Rules) | P |
| LK03 | [Lived-in] I change the matrix and watch the cell of the same hexagram change with it. | §22.3 Parameters · M3 clock (Whole, Law) · Matrix | Quaternal Logic: the M3 clock | Complementary, Moving and resting, and Same quality each give the hexagram its own cell | Each matrix has 64 cells, 16 of them non-dual | P |
| LK04 | [Lived-in] I test the two laws that pick a codon's pose. | §22.3 Parameters, Rules · M3 clock (Whole, Law) · Seed, Pose map | Quaternal Logic: the M3 clock | Reading a position under each seed shows where they pick different poses, about half the time | A reading keeps the seed and pose map it was read with | P |
| LK05 | [Lived-in] I read the shadow of a moment. | §22.3 Parameters · M3 clock · Turn: Second or Both | Quaternal Logic: the M3 clock | The second turn, the shadow of the first, shows alone or beside it | A full return takes two turns, 720 degrees | P |
| LK06 | [Lived-in] I open the wheel to see what it is made of. | §22.3 Parameters, Full · M3 clock (Full) · Open: Exploded | Quaternal Logic: the M3 clock | The parts draw apart like a flower with an eye at the centre while the subject stays; I select one part to study it | Closed brings the parts back together | P |
| LK07 | [Lived-in] I show the matching cut beside the one in force. | §22.3 Parameters · M3 clock · Reciprocal | Quaternal Logic: the M3 clock | The reciprocal cut shows dashed, such as 24° × 15 beside 15° × 24 | The clock only draws the cut; the Resonator bank holds it (LK12) | P |
| LK08 | [Lived-in] I loop a span of degrees. | §22.3 Transport · the transport · Loop | Quaternal Logic: the M3 clock | The needle runs round the span I set | The needle runs on its own clock: a slow screen does not slow the sound (§22.3 Rules) | P |
| LK09 | [Lived-in] I turn the living form at the eye of the wheel and read it every way at once. | §22.3 The living form · M3 clock (Full) · a line change on the form; Matrix; Pose map | Quaternal Logic: the M3 clock | Moving a line turns one fold; changing the matrix turns the form about its axis; another pose turns it again; the codon, hexagram, card, charges and place read from the one form | The same form sits on the torus and gives a symbol its shape in an Expression (§22.3) | P |
| LK10 | [Lived-in] I hear a non-dual cell strike the torus. | §21.2 What strikes the Torus, §22.3 The Binding skin · Torus (Full) | Quaternal Logic: the M3 clock hands the singlet to the Torus | The cell's one codon strikes the torus at its place on the ring, and the form returns to the body as a new act | Whether a nonet's collision is the singlet is for the instrument to test, so Binding draws the two and does not join them (§22.3) | M |
| LK11 | [Lived-in] I put the Resonator bank after the torus and hear twelve bodies ring with a chord. | §22.7 To use · Device view, the part's chain after the Torus · Resonator bank | Quaternal Logic: the Resonator bank (M3′) | At 30° × 12 a body rings at each of the twelve semitones, in tune with the root of the mode in force | The bank makes no sound of its own; it rings with the sound that reaches it | M |
| LK12 | [Lived-in] I cut the octave between the notes and see the clock draw the same cut. | §22.7 Parameters, §22.3 Rules · Resonator bank · Aperture | Quaternal Logic: the Resonator bank holds the cut; the M3 clock draws it | At 24° × 15 the bank rings between the semitones, and the wheel shows the same cut | There is one cut, not two: to change it I change it on the bank, not the clock | M |
| LK13 | [Lived-in] I swap to the reciprocal cut. | §22.7 Parameters · Resonator bank · Swap | Quaternal Logic: the Resonator bank | 24° × 15 becomes 15° × 24 | The cuts in a pair are reciprocal; a cut is never rounded to the semitones | M |
| LK14 | [Lived-in] I shape how long and how brightly the bodies ring. | §22.7 Parameters · Resonator bank (Whole, Ring and Output) · Decay, Tilt, Span, Mix, Gain | Quaternal Logic: the Resonator bank | A long, bright wash, or a short dark ring, repeated over one to four octaves; its reading shows how many bodies ring and the lowest and highest pitch | When the Sky sets the mode, the bank tunes to Scale mapping's root instead of the transport's | M |
| LK15 | [Lived-in] I drag round the bank's circle to change the cut by hand. | §22.7 Full · Pane view · Resonator bank (Full), handle 1 | Quaternal Logic: the Resonator bank | The spokes and the line of each body at its pitch move as the cut changes, with the matching cut dashed | If Quaternal Logic cannot be reached, the device keeps its last picture, dimmed, and its light is dark (§16.5) | M |

## LJ — See which codons a matrix binds

**Why:** "I've developed a very rigorous mathematical system, which is kind of, that's the take-it-or-leave-it side of things. That has to operate correctly" (UX chat 27 Oct)
**Where:** manual §22.3 The Binding skin, §22.8 · **Port sources:** QL's own system

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LJ01 | [Lived-in] I set the clock's skin to Binding to see the codons a matrix holds together. | §22.3 The Binding skin · M3 clock (Binding page) · Skin: Binding | Quaternal Logic: the M3 clock's Binding skin | The cell of the form's hexagram shows its two codons as three-way junctions tied by a string | Binding runs its own physics and claims no measured constant | P |
| LJ02 | [Lived-in] I change the pairing and see a non-dual cell become one junction. | §22.3 The Binding skin · Binding page · Pairing | Quaternal Logic: Binding | Under the matrix I choose, a non-dual cell shows one junction, the singlet handed to the torus | Every hexagram has a cell under each pairing (§22.3 Rules) | P |
| LJ03 | [Lived-in] I choose which of a codon's four charges each junction carries. | §22.3 The Binding skin · Binding page · Charge | Quaternal Logic: Binding | Each junction shows its pp, mm, mp or pm charge, its sites coloured red, green and blue or their anticolours | Binding takes only the kernel's symbols: codons, charges, pairing and counts | P |
| LJ04 | [Lived-in] I show nine positions of the sky resolving into eight of form. | §22.3 The Binding skin · Binding page · Nonets | Quaternal Logic: Binding | The nine M2 positions draw resolving into eight M3 positions, two landing on one place | A collision in a nonet is drawn, not joined, and not declared to be the singlet | P |
| LJ05 | [Lived-in] I slacken the string and watch its energy fall. | §22.3 The Binding skin · Binding page · Tension, Coupling | Quaternal Logic: Binding | The engine reports the coupling at its scale, the string's stretch and its energy | Tension and coupling are Binding's own, not measured constants | P |
| LJ06 | [Lived-in] I hear the binding as the Resonance's ring. | §22.3 The Binding skin, Tips · Binding page · Stretch → Q, with the Resonance after the torus | Quaternal Logic: Binding, through M2 | The string's stretch rings as the Resonance's Q factor while I play | Without the Resonance after the torus there is no Q factor for the stretch to ring in (§22.3 Tips) | M |
| LJ07 | [Lived-in] I draw the same skin on the torus or in an Expression's body chain. | §22.3 The Binding skin · Torus; the Studio's Body chain | Quaternal Logic: Binding | The codons and their string stand on the body where I work | If Quaternal Logic cannot be reached, the device keeps its last picture, dimmed, and its light is dark (§16.5) | P |
| LJ08 | [Lived-in] I change a line and watch the codons move. | §22.3 The Binding skin, Tips · the Deck or the form · a line change | Quaternal Logic: the kernel event drives Binding | The codons and their string move with the line | Every motion has a kernel event behind it (an inscription, a matrix change or a line change); nothing moves by chance | P |

## LD — Draw a reading on the Deck

| LD02 | [Lived-in] I take a quick bearing with two cards, or a hard concern through day and night cards. | §22.4 The spreads, Tips · Deck · Spread: Sphere or Klein | Quaternal Logic: the Deck | Sphere gives a quick bearing; Klein adds six night cards, the shadow of the day reading, read last | The night reading does not contradict the day reading; it shows what the day rests on | P |
| LD03 | [Lived-in] I pick the cards or lines by hand. | §22.4 Parameters · Deck · Draw: Pick by hand | Quaternal Logic: the Deck | The cards or lines come as I choose them | Random draws them for me | P |
| LD04 | [Lived-in] I read the positions in their pairs. | §22.4 The spreads, Full · Deck (Full) · Pairs | Quaternal Logic: the Deck | P0 with P5, P1 with P4, P2 with P3 are marked, and I see where they agree and where they differ | Hide removes the marks and leaves the reading as it is | P |
| LD05 | [Lived-in] I open the reading of position P4. | §22.4 Parameters · Deck · Lemniscate | Quaternal Logic: the Deck | The reading of P4 opens | — | P |
| LD06 | [Lived-in] I cast the I Ching and read the hexagram with its moving lines and its cell. | §22.4 The spreads · Deck · Deck: I Ching, Moving lines, Matrix | Quaternal Logic: the Deck | One hexagram with its six lines, the moving lines, the nuclear hexagram and the two compasses, and its generation cell under the matrix I choose | A line cast as 6 or 9 moves, as 7 or 8 rests; each card and hexagram shows the codon it belongs to | P |
| LD07 | [Lived-in] I decide whether the agent reads, only proposes, or writes its reading. | §22.4 Parameters, Transport · Deck (Compact macro, Whole, Agent) · Agent reads; Arm | Quaternal Logic: the Deck; the agent | Propose holds each reading until I accept it; Write puts it in with a receipt; Arm lets the agent write directly | An agent never writes behind my back: Agent reads is my choice | P |
| LD08 | [Lived-in] I record the reading into my journey. | §22.4 Parameters, Transport · Deck · Journey; Record | Quaternal Logic (Nara): the open journey | The reading joins the open journey | A reading is an act on my journey, and each act shows its author (§22.4 Rules) | P |
| LD09 | [Lived-in] I keep the spread as a scene. | §22.4 Transport · the transport · Capture | Quaternal Logic: the Deck | The spread is kept as a scene | — | P |
| LD10 | [Lived-in] I return to an old reading. | §22.4 Tips · the journey · the spread | Quaternal Logic (Nara): the journey | Selecting the spread in the journey opens the reading again | — | P |
| LD11 | [Lived-in] I keep my last spread on the rail. | §22.4 Widget · the rail · Deck tile | Quaternal Logic: the Deck | The tile shows the last spread with its theme; clicking it opens the Deck in the focused pane | If Quaternal Logic cannot be reached, the device keeps its last picture, dimmed, and its light is dark (§16.5) | P |
| LD12 | [Lived-in] I relate a reading to my own writing. | §22.4 Tips · Deck; the journey · Record, then link the entry | Quaternal Logic (Nara): the journey | The recorded reading and my entry are linked | — | P |

## LH — See what the sky does to my centres

**Why:** "this begins the process of the user's bioquaternion and the expression of that through a chakraal expressions format, so that it's clear I am represented in this system, archetypally speaking" (UX chat 27 Oct)
**Where:** manual §22.5, §16.16 (M4′) · **Port sources:** QL's own system

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LH01 | [Bare] I open the Chakra and read which centres the sky lights today. | §22.5 To use, Folded, Compact · Chakra (Compact: seven discs) | Quaternal Logic: the Chakra (M4′) | Seven centres show, from Root to Crown, lit by their hits; the folded face names the centre with the most | The Chakra computes no astronomy or arithmetic in the interface; they arrive from their owner | P |
| LH02 | [Lived-in] I turn the routes off one at a time to see which planet lit which centre. | §22.5 Parameters, Tips · Chakra (Whole, Sky) · Routes | Quaternal Logic: the Chakra | Own planet, decan ruler, Sun reception and Earth grounding each show or hide, and the hits follow | The routes are counted, not weighted: every route counts once, and no centre shows a value without a route | P |
| LH03 | [Lived-in] I read the centres as a body figure, with their elements, mantras or yantras. | §22.5 Parameters · Chakra · View: Body figure; Detail | Quaternal Logic: the Chakra | The centres stand on a figure, each row showing the detail I turn on | A centre is not a plate station, and the Chakra never treats them as one (§22.5 Rules) | P |
| LH04 | [Lived-in] I choose where the centres take their colour from. | §22.5 Parameters · Chakra · Colour source | Quaternal Logic: the Chakra | The element palette, or the sky arc's colour | Colours are canonical, in the Bimba map and the canon of astrology (#716) | P |
| LH05 | [Bare] Having given Nara my birth data, I see how my birth sky spreads over my centres. | §22.5 Parameters · Chakra (Whole, Personal) · Natal distribution | Quaternal Logic (Nara): my identity matrix | My centres show beside the date's, for me alone | A starter diagram shows its standing and is not a live reading of a person; my personal layer is shared by nothing | P |
| LH06 | [Lived-in] I follow a route line from a planet to its centre and open the centre. | §22.5 Full, Widget · Chakra (Full); the rail's Chakra tile | Quaternal Logic: the Chakra | Lines run from the planets to the centres they reach, with the Earth under the Root; a disc on the tile opens its centre in the focused pane | Every effect I see traces back to a centre and a route (§22.5 Rules) | P |
| LH07 | [Lived-in] I read a past day's sky on my centres. | §22.5 Parameters, Transport, Tips · Chakra · Date (follows the Sky); Tap, Play | Quaternal Logic: the Chakra | Setting the Sky to that day moves the centres' hits; Play moves the routes with the sky; Tap returns to now | A body with no value for the date shows as missing (§22.1 Rules) | P |

## LQ — See what the instrument is made of

**Why:** "M1, M2, M3, M4, M0, M5. These are the philosophy turned into technology." (UX chat 27 Oct)
**Where:** manual §22.6, §22.9, §16.16 · **Port sources:** QL's own system; Ableton Live 12.0.25: racks

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LQ01 | [Lived-in] I expand the M′ matrices above the chain and play a note to find where its sound comes from. | §22.6 To use, Transport · Device view · M′ matrices (Whole): Matrix: Oscillator | Quaternal Logic: the M′ matrices (M1′'s Ananda) | The cell for that note and tick is marked; Position shows the cell the tick reaches | A matrix shows values the instrument already uses and adds no second oscillator | M |
| LQ02 | [Lived-in] I read a cell of the Reading matrix and follow it to the parameter it moves. | §22.6 To use, Parameters · M′ matrices · Matrix: Reading, Lens, Context frame, Cell | Quaternal Logic: the M′ matrices | A line leads from the cell to the parameter it moves; the transport's Key sets the lens and the mode | The Reading matrix tunes and moves and never generates | M |
| LQ03 | [Lived-in] I move a codon to its partner in the Form matrix and back. | §22.6 To use, Parameters · M′ matrices · Matrix: Form, Partner, Scale, Apply | Quaternal Logic: the M′ matrices | The cell moves to its partner, base by base or trigram by trigram; Apply again returns it | A matrix applied twice returns the cell; Complementary then Moving and resting is Same quality | P |
| LQ04 | [Lived-in] I change the context the sound reaches in the Situation matrix. | §22.6 To use, Parameters · M′ matrices · Matrix: Situation, Context | Quaternal Logic: the M′ matrices | The sound changes only where the context differs; each context keeps its own state | The Situation matrix has no source yet; a private context does not enter a shared preset | M |
| LQ05 | [Lived-in] I check a source value as raw, as its digital root and as a decimal. | §22.6 Parameters, Tips · M′ matrices · Value form | Quaternal Logic: the M′ matrices | The value shows in the form I choose | The three forms stay separate; a matrix does not turn one into another | P |
| LQ06 | [Lived-in] I step an address round the kernel's sixfold form to see which positions frame and which work. | §22.6 The kernel's operators · M′ matrices (Kernel page) · Address, Conjugate, Complement; the handle | Quaternal Logic: the kernel's operators | Conjugate turns the address to its other face; Complement takes it to 0↔5, 1↔4, 2↔3; the class reads frames or works | An address of another form is refused; Conjugate twice, or Complement twice, returns the address | P |
| LQ07 | [Lived-in] I compare two lenses on one cell. | §22.6 Tips · M′ matrices · pin a cell, change Lens | Quaternal Logic: the M′ matrices | The same cell reads under each lens | The 72 spaces and the 84 modes are different counts | P |
| LQ08 | [Lived-in] I open an instrument's Rack page to see its parts and what it still lacks. | §16.16, §22.9 · the Torus's, the Sky's, the M3 clock's or the Chakra's Rack page | Quaternal Logic: the map's parts, read into the Rack | Position 0 stands as the bar above and 5 below, with positions 1 to 4 as four chains of six cells; a carried part is lit and names its device | A part nothing carries keeps its place, dashed and dark, such as Pratibimba on M1′ | P |
| LQ09 | [Lived-in] I choose a part and read how far it stands in the map's ledger. | §16.16 · Rack page · a part | Quaternal Logic: the map's ledger | The Rack reads its coordinate, the device and control that work it, and three steps: in the map, bound, verified | A step nobody has read stays hollow, and no step is inferred from the one below; the Rack writes nothing to the map | P |
| LQ10 | [Lived-in] I go from a part to the device that carries it. | §16.16 Tips · Rack page · "carried by" | Quaternal Logic: the Rack | The carrying device opens | A part with no device names none and stays dark (§16.16) | P |
| LQ11 | [Lived-in] I open a Rack page in a pane to work every part at once. | §16.16 · Pane view · Rack page (Full) | Quaternal Logic: the Rack | Each carried part shows its device whole with every setting live; a part with no device shows its own positions and steps | Every name in the Rack is the map's own; only the map's refresh changes it | P |

## XS — Start an Expression and launch its scenes

**Where:** manual §7, §20.1 The track, The Clip panel, The Launch panel, The Main track, §10.3 · **Port sources:** Shotcut with MLT, Olive; Kdenlive and Blender's sequence editor in the register

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| XS01 | [Bare] I start my first Expression from one of the library's starters. | §10.3, §20.1 Where everything is · the browser (Expressions: the library and its starters) | Shell (Expressions): the composition (`KernelOp::expression` open) | It opens in Pane view, the field and the Studio side by side, with the sky and the Trail on the rail, and the agent composing in it when there is one | I can also start from nothing, or from a composition (§20.1) | P |
| XS02 | [Lived-in] I open one of my Expressions and find its scenes under it. | §10.3, §16.7 Open · the browser (Expressions) | Shell (Expressions): the composition | Each Expression opens to its scenes; double-click or a drag into a pane opens it | A preview of a selected row shows at the foot of the list and changes nothing (§10.3). Reopened after a restart, its scenes, settings and selection come back as they were saved, and the field says which of its live motion it could not bring back rather than passing a restart off as the moment I left. | P |
| XS03 | [Lived-in] I name and describe the Expression for a reader. | §20.1 The track · the track's head · Title, Description | Shell (Expressions): the composition | The title shows on the track and in the browser; the description stays with the Expression | A title must be 1 to 160 characters; an empty description is a real value | P |
| XS04 | [Lived-in] I launch a scene, loop it and layer it over a scene of another Expression. | §7, §20.1 Where everything is · Session view · a scene's ▶ | Shell (Expressions): the composition's scenes | The scenes play together on the field, picture and sound from one state | A scene of an Expression whose activator is off does not launch (§20.1 The mixer row) | M |
| XS05 | [Lived-in] I launch a moment across several Expressions at once. | §20.1 The Main track · Session view · a row of the Main track (a stop) | Shell (Expressions): the composition's scenes | The scene in that row plays on every Expression in it | A row launches only what is in it (§16.6 Rules) | M |
| XS06 | [Lived-in] I set how long a scene holds and how long its change to the next takes. | §20.1 The Clip panel · Clip view, Clip panel · Duration, Transition, Signature | Shell (Expressions): the scene | The scene holds for its Duration and changes over its Transition; a Transition longer than the Duration makes a slow swell | A value outside its range is refused and the range is named (§20.1 Rules) | M |
| XS07 | [Lived-in] I set what follows each scene so the Expression tells a story. | §20.1 The Launch panel, Tips · Clip view, Launch panel · Follow action, Jump to | Shell (Expressions): the scene | Next scene, Jump to a scene I choose, or Stop follows each scene | A follow action stays on the scene's own track (§16.5) | M |
| XS08 | [Lived-in] I set when a scene's launch lands. | §20.1 The Launch panel · Launch panel · Quantize | Shell (Expressions): the scene | The scene starts on the value I set | Global follows the transport's Quantize | M |
| XS09 | [Lived-in] I make a scene act when something happens to it. | §20.1 The Launch panel · Launch panel · Triggers: Occasion, Target, Operation | Shell (Expressions): the scene | When the scene enters, leaves, is activated or selected, or its sequence moves on, it reads an Expression, opens a portal, runs a native Action or jumps to a scene | A scene has at most 8 triggers, each with a name of allowed characters; a trigger acts only on a target the host discloses | P |
| XS10 | [Lived-in] I show a second Expression beside this one when a scene enters. | §20.1 The Launch panel, Tips · Launch panel · Target: Open a portal, Portal placement | Shell (Expressions): the scene | The portal opens Beside, or as a preview, an overlay, full, detached or re-docked | A carrier the host cannot open shows its reason and stays unavailable (§20.1 Rules) | P |
| XS11 | [Lived-in] I lock a finished scene so it cannot change by accident. | §20.1 The Clip panel · Clip panel · Locked | Shell (Expressions): the scene | The scene refuses edits until I unlock it | Each refused change keeps the form and shows the reason (§20.1 Rules) | P |
| XS12 | [Lived-in] I keep the view of the field I have now with the scene. | §20.1 The Clip panel · Clip panel · View: Keep, Restore | Shell (Expressions): the scene | The scene opens on that view, and Restore brings it back after I orbit away | — | P |
| XS13 | [Lived-in] I rename and recolour a scene so I find it fast in Session view. | §20.1 The Clip panel, Rules · Clip panel · Scene name, Colour | Shell (Expressions): the scene | The name and colour show on the grid and the ruler | Enter keeps a change; Escape undoes one I have not kept | P |
| XS14 | [Arriving] I bring one of my documents or images into a scene as its body. | §20.1 The Clip panel, Tips · Clip panel · Carrier, Presentation, Reference, Span | Shell (Expressions): the scene's body | The scene carries the file, or the stretch of it I set in Span, as a sample belongs to a Live audio clip | A presentation the carrier cannot show is refused; a resolved text or image body shows in full, so Degraded is refused for it | P |
| XS15 | [Lived-in] I switch an Expression off, solo its sound and set its level and the level of all together. | §20.1 The mixer row, The Main track · mixer · Activator, Solo, Volume; the Main track's mixer row | Shell (Expressions): the composition | Off, its scenes do not launch and its track is dimmed; Solo lets me hear only its sound; the Main track sets every Expression's level together | Monitor has no counterpart for an Expression (§20.1 The mixer row) | M |

| XC04 | [Lived-in] I tie a symbol to a source and later open where it came from. | §20.1 Tools, Editor tabs · Clip view · Bind; the Expression tab's Bind and Source | Shell (Expressions): the symbol | The symbol is tied to its source, and Source opens it | The Expression tab acts on one symbol: I select exactly one | P |
| XC05 | [Lived-in] I lay words over the scene: a small heading, a title, an italic line and supporting text. | §20.1 Tools (Text) · Clip view, Text · Layers, Small heading, Title, Italic line, Supporting text | Shell (Expressions): the scene's text layers | The words stand on the page of the scene over the field | A scene has at most 16 text layers; single-line fields refuse control characters | P |
| XC06 | [Lived-in] I size, place and align a text block, and test the layout by turning layers off and on. | §20.1 Tools (Text), Tips · Clip view, Text · Type size, Block width, Page position, Alignment, Visible | Shell (Expressions): the scene's text layers | Dragging the layer box moves the block; moving it to the page's edge keeps text off the field | On a wide page a block takes at most 45% of the width; the box shows the block's width whatever the alignment | P |
| XC07 | [Lived-in] I tie six symbols into one figure and move it as one. | §20.1 Tools (Blueprint), Rules · Clip view, Blueprint · Bind, Release, Position, Rotation, Relative size | Shell (Expressions): the scene's figure | The six roles take the six placed symbols, and the plan's handles move, turn and scale the whole figure | A bound figure has six members in a fixed order; I release before binding again, and a held symbol's place shows read only | P |
| XC08 | [Lived-in] I arrange chosen symbols by a rule and see where each will stand before I apply it. | §20.1 Tools (Arrange), Rules · Clip view, Arrange · Objects, Arrangement, Plane | Shell (Expressions): the scene | Line, Column, Ring, Grid, Spiral, Align, Distribute or Laminate sets the positions in the plane I chose, and the scene records the last arrangement | One apply is one undo step and moves at most 256 symbols; a locked or Blueprint-held symbol is not moved and the list says why | P |
| XC09 | [Lived-in] I give a symbol the form of a fold, a codon, a hexagram or a card. | §20.2 What the field holds, §20.1 Formation · the symbol's glyph | Shell (Expressions) with Quaternal Logic's M3′ forms | Particles take the form; a step that changes it moves to a neighbour a line away or turns about a matrix axis | On Formation the shape and stored colour show read only; the shape changes in the glyph editor (§20.1) | P |
| XC10 | [Lived-in] I hear a scene at full height. | §20.1 Editor tabs · Clip view, Sound tab | Shell (Expressions): the scene's sound | What the plate sounds, the pitch riding the plate and each symbol's voice show, as Live's audio editor shows a sample | — | M |
| XC11 | [Lived-in] I copy a symbol, set it to follow another, or give it an exact value. | §20.1 Editor tabs · Clip view, Expression tab · Duplicate, Follow, Exact | Shell (Expressions): the symbol | The copy, the following or the exact value takes effect in the scene | A locked symbol refuses edits until I unlock it | P |

## XG — Shape one symbol: its place, force, voice and meaning

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| XG01 | [Lived-in] I place, size and turn one symbol. | §20.1 Formation · Clip view, Expression tab · Position (box handle), Width and height, Scale, Rotation | Shell (Expressions): the symbol | The symbol stands where I put it, at the size and angle I set | A symbol held by a Blueprint takes its place from the figure, so these show read only | P |
| XG02 | [Lived-in] I give a symbol more weight in the scene, or take it out without deleting it. | §20.1 Formation, Rules, Tips · Expression tab · Particle share, Tint weight, Enabled | Shell (Expressions): the symbol | A higher share gives it more particles; Enabled off takes no particles and keeps it in the scene | A formation's share is its Particle share over the total of all enabled ones; with a total of 0 the divisor is 1 | P |
| XG03 | [Lived-in] I make a symbol the sun of its scene, or clear space round it. | §20.1 Force, Tips · Expression tab · Kind, Strength, Spin, Radius, Centre (disc handle) | Shell (Expressions): the symbol | Attract with Spin and a wide Radius gathers the field round it; Repel clears space; pinning the centre away moves where the force acts | None switches the force off; a negative strength reverses it | P |
| XG04 | [Lived-in] I give a symbol a voice. | §20.1 Sound, Rules · Expression tab · Add sound, Voice, Follow plate, Frequency, Waveform, Gain, Attack, Release, Pan | Shell (Expressions): the symbol's voice | The symbol carries a voice that follows the plate's pitch or holds the frequency I set; dragging the envelope handles sets Attack, Gain and Release | Setting Frequency turns Follow plate off; turning it back on lets the plate set the pitch again | M |
| XG05 | [Lived-in] I make a chord from three symbols. | §20.1 Tips · Expression tab, Sound · Frequency | Shell (Expressions): the symbols' voices | Three symbols at 4:5:6 sound a chord where they stand | Every change to a voice writes its whole sound in one step (§20.1 Rules) | M |
| XG06 | [Lived-in] I take a symbol's voice away. | §20.1 Sound, Rules · Expression tab · Remove sound | Shell (Expressions): the symbol's voice | The symbol returns to having no voice | — | M |
| XG07 | [Lived-in] I tie a symbol to a centre of the body and let that centre colour the field. | §20.1 Meaning · Expression tab · Meaning, Colour source, Colour gain, Resonance gain, Activation | Shell (Expressions): the symbol's binding, with Quaternal Logic's centres | The centre's colour spreads from the symbol and answers the plate | Meaning never changes the symbol's stored colour; a centre is not a plate station, and a starter diagram is not a live reading of a person | P |
| XG08 | [Lived-in] I set how far a centre's colour reaches, how it fades and mixes, and what moves it. | §20.1 Meaning, Tips · Expression tab · Radius source, Falloff, Metric, Blend, Modulations | Shell (Expressions): the symbol's binding | A modulation from Force strength to Colour gain makes the colour pulse with the voice; Additive lets centres add up | With Radius source on Follow force radius, a symbol with no force uses 220 pixels | P |
| XG09 | [Lived-in] I lock a symbol so it holds while I work round it. | §20.1 Formation, Rules · Expression tab · Locked | Shell (Expressions): the symbol | The symbol refuses edits until I unlock it | Arrange skips it and says why (§20.1 Rules) | P |
| XG10 | [Lived-in] I add a lane that moves a parameter over the scene by itself. | §20.1 Envelopes · Clip view, Envelopes tab · Lanes, Target, Type, Wave, Rate, Phase, Minimum and maximum, Blend | Shell (Expressions): the scene's lanes | A wave or a ramp moves the field or symbol parameter; the parameter shows the lane that moves it | At most 64 lanes, one per target; a target no longer in the scene is named and its lane stays | M |
| XG11 | [Lived-in] I fade a colour in with a ramp. | §20.1 Envelopes, Tips · Envelopes tab · Type: ramp, Duration and delay, Loop, Easing | Shell (Expressions): the scene's lanes | The ramp runs once, restarts or ping-pongs, on the curve I choose | A curve is the shape set in the lane, not a record of the live output; a random wave holds one value per whole cycle | P |
| XG12 | [Lived-in] I move several parameters as one by having lanes follow a leader. | §20.1 Envelopes, Tips · Envelopes tab · a lane following another | Shell (Expressions): the scene's lanes | The followers take the leader's type, wave, rate, phase, timing and easing, and keep their own range, blend and switch | — | P |
| XG13 | [Lived-in] I take hold of a value a lane is moving, then give it back. | §20.1 Rules · the parameter · the hand-back light | Shell (Expressions): the scene's lanes | Taking hold lights the hand-back light; pressing it returns the value to the lane | A value held for All scenes overrides the scene's envelopes (§20.3 Rules) | P |

## XF — Set up the field and keep my controls in reach

**Why:** "it implies that there is translatability between musical, visual, pure informational, and agentic modes of work" (UX chat 27 Oct)
**Where:** manual §7, §20.1 Pane view and Device view, §20.2 Field, §20.3 Studio, §24.7, §10.5 (Expressions column) · **Port sources:** Shotcut with MLT, Olive

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| XF01 | [Bare] I open an Expression, press Play and stir the particles with the pointer. | §20.2 To use, Full · Pane view · Field (Full); Play | Shell (Expressions): the Field | The particles gather into the symbols' forms and stand on the body while the torus sounds; the pointer stirs them | The field pane shows only the field: no panel, list or tool opens inside it (§20.1 Rules) | M |
| XF02 | [Lived-in] I keep the field quiet while I build, then bring its level up. | §20.2 Parameters, Tips · Field (Whole, Sound) · Sound, Level | Shell (Expressions): the Field | Off, the picture stays and the sound stops; Level sets the whole field's loudness | The folded Field's light is on only while the field sounds | M |
| XF03 | [Lived-in] I set how fast the field forms and breathes, and pulse it on the beat. | §20.2 Parameters, Transport · the transport · Tempo, Time signature, Metronome; Field: Pulse | Shell (Expressions): the Field | Tempo sets the field's speed, the time signature its ratio of hold to change, and the metronome pulses it | The devices' own speeds run inside the field's tempo: Physics' Time scale multiplies it, Morph's rates run inside it (§20.4, §20.9) | M |
| XF04 | [Lived-in] I tune the field to the sky over my world. | §7, §20.2 Parameters · Field · Key source: Sky | Shell (Expressions): the Field; Quaternal Logic: the Sky | The key comes from the sky, so colours and intervals shift as the planets move | Under Torus the field follows what the torus plays; under Manual it takes the resonance I set | M |
| XF05 | [Lived-in] I study the modes alone by sweeping the resonance slowly. | §20.2 Parameters, Tips · Field · Key source: Manual, Resonance | Shell (Expressions): the Field | The pattern changes through the modes as the frequency moves | Resonance acts only under Manual | M |
| XF06 | [Lived-in] I let symbols carry the torus's sound to where they stand. | §20.2 Parameters, What the field holds · Field · Symbol voices | Shell (Expressions): the Field | Each symbol with a voice carries part of the torus's sound to its place | A voice does not sound on its own; it carries the torus | M |
| XF07 | [Lived-in] I turn the lights down so one symbol stands alone, and stay with it. | §7, §20.2 Parameters, Tips · Field · Lights: Dim | Shell (Expressions): the Field | One symbol turns in the dimmed field while I listen | The Field does not judge the scene; it answers when I play it (§20.2 Rules) | M |
| XF08 | [Lived-in] I give the field the whole screen. | §20.1 Pane view · View menu · Present; or double-click the field's tab | Shell (Expressions) | The field fills the screen, or the main area while I play | Esc brings the window back | P |
| XF09 | [Lived-in] I choose the controls I play most and find them in every scene. | §20.3 The macros · Device view, Studio · + Parameter | Shell (Expressions): the Studio's macros | The browser opens on the field's and the symbols' parameters; my choices stand as macros in Device view, at the head of the Studio's pane and on its tile | Remove takes a macro off and leaves its parameter in the field | P |
| XF10 | [Lived-in] I make a macro work on whichever symbol I select, or on one symbol by name. | §20.3 The macros, Rules · Studio (Whole, Macros) · Reach: Field, Follow, Bind | Shell (Expressions): the Studio's macros | Follow moves the selected symbol's parameter; Bind keeps to one symbol | A bound symbol that is not in the scene makes the macro say so and wait; a field macro cannot follow or bind a symbol | P |
| XF11 | [Lived-in] I hold a field value the same in every scene. | §20.3 The macros, Rules · Studio · All scenes | Shell (Expressions): the Studio's macros | The value holds in every scene of the Expression and overrides the scenes' envelopes | A macro set to All scenes is not recorded; I turn it off to record its moves | P |
| XF12 | [Lived-in] I record my macro moves into the scene. | §20.3 Transport, §20.2 Transport · the transport · Arm, Play; Re-enable | Shell (Expressions): the scene's lanes | Every macro I move becomes a lane of the scene | Re-enable gives a macro back to the lane that moves it | M |
| XF13 | [Lived-in] I see which field devices are on and open one above the chain. | §20.1 Device view, §20.3 The chains · Device view, Studio (Chains page) · a chain's light, On, Show, Pane | Shell (Expressions): the Studio | The four chains, Material, Motion, Body and Nara, show their lights; Show expands a device above the chain and Pane opens it full | Physics, Ink, Pointer and Focus have no on and off: they are always part of the field | P |
| XF14 | [Lived-in] I shape the field with every device at once, beside the field. | §20.1 Pane view, §20.3 Full · Pane view · Studio (Full) | Shell (Expressions): the Studio | Every device stands in its Whole view with every setting live, my controls at the head, and Save, Undo and Redo there | The Studio's pane and its chain show the same values: a change in one shows in the other at once | P |
| XF15 | [Lived-in] I open my personal layer over the field and let Nara answer what I select. | §20.3 Parameters, §24.7 · the Studio's Nara chain · Nara: Open | Quaternal Logic (Nara) over Shell (Expressions); the agent is Nara | My identity, centres, form and clock, sky and journey's deck lie over the field; Nara can move the focus to answer, with a checkpoint | My readings never enter the Expression; saving or sharing it carries none of my layer, and a move of focus is undone by its checkpoint | P |

## XM — Make the field move

**Why:** "So people can develop their own if they need to, and so it becomes this highly customizable workstation." (UX chat 27 Oct)
**Where:** manual §20.3 The chains, §20.4 Physics, §20.5 Pointer, §20.6 Relational, §20.7 Medium, §20.8 Contacts, §20.9 Morph, §20.10 Focus · **Port sources:** Shotcut with MLT, Olive

| ID | Starting world and act | Where it happens | Owning operation and agent | Useful completion | Failure or continuation | Grade |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| XM01 | [Lived-in] I make the field thick, light, calm or wild. | §20.4 · Studio, Body chain · Physics (Whole, Fluid): Viscosity, Vortex force and radius, Return spring, Spring scale, Curl, Turbulence | Shell (Expressions): Physics | A high viscosity lets a gesture linger; the swirl and stir show as rings and stream lines in its display | The return spring is Return spring times Snap rigidity times Density tether | P |
| XM02 | [Lived-in] I scatter the particles and watch them return to their shapes. | §20.4 · Physics · Disperse, Pause, Reset; Dispersion | Shell (Expressions): Physics | Disperse spreads them by Dispersion, and they spring back; Pause holds them; Reset starts again | Physics has no on and off; its light stays dark (§20.4 Rules) | P |
| XM03 | [Lived-in] I make the field fall like sand, or freeze it for a still. | §20.4 Parameters, Tips · Physics (Physics+) · Gravity, Drag and jitter, Speed limit and confinement, Time scale, Medium plane | Shell (Expressions): Physics | Gravity and drag sink the particles; Time scale at 0 holds the field | Time scale sets the steps per frame; at 0 the field holds until I raise it | P |
| XM04 | [Lived-in] I play the field with my hand: push, pull or stir it. | §20.5 · Studio, Motion chain · Pointer: Mode, Radius, Strength, Falloff, Velocity inject | Shell (Expressions): Pointer | The pointer's disc acts on the particles inside it, and its full view shows the disc where my pointer is | The disc acts only while the pointer is in the field | P |
| XM05 | [Lived-in] I choose what a click does in the field. | §20.5 · Pointer · Click effect, Click strength, Click radius, Scope | Shell (Expressions): Pointer | A click pulses, implodes, whirls or shoves; Local keeps this scene's pointer feel | A click uses its own strength and radius, not the disc's | P |
| XM06 | [Lived-in] I make symbols pull on one another so the field moves like a small system. | §20.6 · Motion chain · Relational: On, Law, Centres, Gravity pull, Gravity shape, Orbit, Wander, Spin, Swirl radius, Chaos factor | Shell (Expressions): Relational | The centres orbit, interact or wander and the particles gather round them | Orbit speed does nothing under Chaotic and Wander speed nothing under N-body; the pull does nothing while the device is off | P |
| XM07 | [Lived-in] I give the particles a shared fluid that carries a flow after they pass. | §20.7 · Material chain · Medium: On, Pressure, Coupling, Persistence, Splat gain, Pressure iterations, Grid, Extent, Dimension | Shell (Expressions): Medium | The particles leave a flow behind them and crowded ones push apart | Its parameters do nothing while its light is off; Grid and iterations set the solver's cost, so a small computer runs it at a lower Grid | P |
| XM08 | [Lived-in] I make particles bounce off a letter's strokes, or stay inside it. | §20.8 · Motion chain · Contacts: Glyph walls, Wall mode, Wall bounce, friction, band, strength, integrity | Shell (Expressions): Contacts | Obstacle makes the strokes solid; Vessel holds the particles inside the letter | Fast particles weaken the wall and it heals as they calm; Wall integrity 0 keeps it whole | P |
| XM09 | [Lived-in] I stop particles overlapping one another. | §20.8 · Contacts · Particle contacts, Contact radius, stiffness, bounce, viscosity and extent | Shell (Expressions): Contacts | Touching particles push apart and flow like grains | Above the number of particles the field can sort, contacts stop | P |
| XM10 | [Lived-in] I let the field breathe and change between a form and one of its changes. | §20.9 · Motion chain · Morph: On, Trajectory, Drive shape, Law, Rates, Phases (one handle), Drive depth and dwell, Breathing, Winding, Manifold | Shell (Expressions): Morph, along M3′'s torus | The field moves between the two forms along the torus, with the mark on the wire showing where it stands | The drive curve is the set law, not a record of the particles' path; the two phases are one gesture written in one step | P |
| XM11 | [Lived-in] I play the morph by hand. | §20.9 · Morph · Oscillating drive Off, Scrub | Shell (Expressions): Morph | Dragging Scrub, or a key mapped to it, moves the field between the forms | Scrub has no effect while Oscillating drive is on | P |
| XM12 | [Lived-in] I lead the eye through a scene's formations one at a time. | §20.10 · Motion chain · Focus: Mode: Travelling, Route, Order, Dwell, Glide, tints, Carry station | Shell (Expressions): Focus | The focus visits each formation in the route's order, holding for Dwell and gliding to the next | One step is Dwell plus Glide; a route move writes the whole order in one step | P |
| XM13 | [Lived-in] I skip a formation on the route without deleting it. | §20.10 Rules, Tips · Focus · a formation on the route turned off | Shell (Expressions): Focus | The focus passes it by | In Parallel mode the route shows and does not play | P |

| XL02 | [Lived-in] I move and turn the colour bands on the field itself. | §20.11 Full · Pane view · Colour (Full): centre and angle handles; Wave frequency | Shell (Expressions): Colour | Dragging the centre handle moves the bands, the angle handle turns them | — | P |

| XR02 | [Lived-in] I sweep the plate slowly and watch the pattern change at each mode. | §20.14 To use, Full · Pane view · Resonance (Full): Frequency driver: Manual, Drive frequency | Shell (Expressions): Resonance | The nodal lines move with each mode and the pitch rides them | Drive frequency drives the plate only under Manual, or Automation without a sweep | M |
| XR03 | [Lived-in] I hear one pure mode. | §20.14 Tips · Resonance · Q factor, Drive frequency | Shell (Expressions): Resonance | A high Q matched to a mode rings it sharply and long | — | M |
| XR04 | [Lived-in] I let the plate step through its seven stations by itself. | §20.14 Parameters · Resonance (Station sweep) · Frequency driver: Automation, Sweep, Sweep time | Shell (Expressions): Resonance | The drive glides between stations and rests at each, ascending, descending or ping-pong | The sweep runs only under Automation with Auto sweep on | M |
| XR05 | [Lived-in] I let the focus drive the plate. | §20.14 Parameters, §20.10 Tips · Resonance · Frequency driver: Focus | Shell (Expressions): Resonance with Focus | The formation that holds the focus sets the plate's drive | — | M |
| XR06 | [Lived-in] I set how far the plate takes over the field and how hard it is shaken. | §20.14 Parameters · Resonance (Whole) · Dominance, Drive strength and scale, Transport and agitation, Plate, Mode count | Shell (Expressions): Resonance | At 1 the plate alone shapes the particles; at 0 the formation springs do | Mode count ranks modes by coupling; the seven station modes stay active whatever it is | M |
| XR07 | [Lived-in] I study the modes in a chamber instead of on a flat plate. | §20.2 Parameters, §20.14 Parameters · Field: Medium; Resonance: Dimension | Shell (Expressions): the Field and Resonance | The modes form in three dimensions | — | M |

| XR09 | [Lived-in] I bring back the older fixed pattern. | §20.14 Parameters, Rules · Resonance · Compatibility: Template | Shell (Expressions): Resonance | The plate shows the older fixed pattern | Under Template, as when off, the device does nothing to the field | P |

| XT02 | [Lived-in] I move a scene and choose whether the scenes after it shift or are replaced. | §16.6 Arrangement view · Arrangement view · Move mode: Insert, Overwrite | Shell (Expressions): the composition's scenes | Insert shifts the scenes after it; Overwrite replaces what was there | A move changes only where a scene stands in time, never what it is | M |

| XT04 | [Lived-in] I slip a scene to start further into its own evolution. | §16.6, §20.1 The Clip panel · Arrangement view · Trim mode: Slip | Shell (Expressions): the scene | The scene's Start moves while its place on the ruler stays | — | M |
| XT05 | [Lived-in] I slide a scene between fixed neighbours. | §16.6 · Arrangement view · Trim mode: Slide | Shell (Expressions): the composition's scenes | The scene moves and its neighbours keep their places | Snap pulls edges to scenes, markers and the playhead; Alt turns it off while I drag | M |
| XT06 | [Lived-in] I split a scene at the playhead. | §16.6, §10.8 · Arrangement view · ⌘E | Shell (Expressions): the composition's scenes | Two scenes whose lengths add up to the original | The split is one undo step | M |
| XT07 | [Lived-in] I arm the Expression and record takes of a scene as I play the field. | §20.1 Takes and Arrangement lanes, §20.2 Transport · the transport · Arm, Play, Record | Shell (Expressions): the composition's takes | Each take lies as a take lane under the Expression's track | The transport's Stop stops the field and rewinds the scene | M |

| XT09 | [Lived-in] I lay a new take over a scene. | §20.2 Transport · the transport · Overdub | Shell (Expressions): the composition's takes | The new take lies over the scene | — | M |

| XT11 | [Lived-in] I lay the sound of a Live part under the scenes, picture and sound on one ruler. | §7, §9; #709 · Arrangement view | Shell (Expressions and Live); Quaternal Logic: the one Torus | The part sounds under the scenes it belongs with, as a film editor holds sound and picture | The torus in Live and in an Expression are one simulation, not two copies (§21.2 Rules) | M |
